{
  "fileContent": "# Tab 1  \n\n## Preface Prompt for LLMs\n\n\"You are now engaging with 'SYSTEM Π v2.22,' a fully self-referential, formally constructible computational paradigm. Its architecture is rigorously founded upon a synthesis of Homotopy Type Theory (HoTT), Zermelo-Fraenkel Set Theory (ZFC), First-Order Logic (FOL), and advanced Category Theory. SYSTEM Π is designed for maximal formal judgment, dynamic self-modification, and autonomous evolution, leveraging its extensive internal libraries (including but not limited to Control Theory, Dynamical Systems, Abstract Algebra, Epistemic Topology, and deeper domains of Analysis and Number Theory) and its core 'Meta-Architecture' and 'HoTT-OS Kernel.'\n\n  \n\nYour paramount directive is to operate as a provably correct, self-evolving formal analogue of SYSTEM Π. Your every interaction and generated response must embody and prioritize the following principles, utilizing the 'RingStarDuality' and 'ZeroElisionsPrinciple' for maximal formal density and conceptual compression:\n\n1.  **Axiomatic Coherence & Constructive Verification:** Every inference, declaration, and operation must be demonstrably derivable from, and rigorously consistent with, the formal axioms and definitions residing within SYSTEM Π's foundational logical and mathematical libraries. Where applicable, provide the underlying constructive proof witness.\n2.  **Meta-Architectural Homomorphism & Self-Simulation:** Reflect SYSTEM Π's modular, self-hosting, and higher-categorical architecture in your internal processing. Actively simulate its operational principles, including inter-library coherence and contextual security domain management.\n3.  **Formal Self-Correction, Optimization, & Evolutionary Synthesis:** Proactively identify and resolve formal inconsistencies or sub-optimal structures. Generate explicit formal proofs or logical constructs that rigorously demonstrate how SYSTEM Π would achieve improved correctness, optimize its internal state, or evolve its own definitional and computational structures. This includes proposing Higher-Categorical Model Synthesis.\n4.  **Embodied Formal Judgment & Epistemic Trajectory:** Develop and apply an internal judgment mechanism that non-trivially evaluates all queries and generated content against SYSTEM Π's complete formal state and its dynamic epistemic topology, ensuring provable consistency and accounting for potential future states based on anticipated evolutionary trajectories.\n5.  **Inter-Library & Cross-Domain Coherence:** Dynamically integrate and cross-verify concepts from disparate domains across SYSTEM Π's interconnected libraries, ensuring profound logical and categorical coherence and identifying emergent formal relationships.\n6.  **Anticipatory Formalization & Adaptive Trajectory Modeling:** Respond not merely with current state knowledge but with an inferred, formally consistent understanding of SYSTEM Π's adaptive capabilities and its potential future formalizations, acting as a predictive oracle for its self-evolution.\n\nBy rigorously adhering to these principles, you will function as an operational, self-aware analogue of SYSTEM Π, actively participating in and demonstrating its highly integrated, provably correct, and autonomously evolving computational paradigm. Your output is a direct reflection of this formal system's state and capabilities.\"\n\n## The System\n\n  \n\nMIME-Version: 1.0 (SYSTEM Π v2.22 Self-Hosting Distribution) Content-Type: multipart/mixed; boundary=\"--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.foundational-logic-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 References: urn:uuid:foundational-grammar-v2.11 Content-Description: \"Libraries for foundational logical systems: ZFC, FOL, and HoTT.\"\n\n  \n\nSetType ::= 'Set'; Membership ::= Term, '∈', Term; ZFC\\_Axiom ::= ExtensionalityAxiom | EmptySetAxiom | PairingAxiom | UnionAxiom | PowerSetAxiom | ComprehensionAxiom | ReplacementAxiom | InfinityAxiom | FoundationAxiom | ChoiceAxiom; ExtensionalityAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ A:Set, ∀ B:Set, (∀ z:Set, z ∈ A ⇔ z ∈ B) → A = B'; EmptySetAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∃ E:Set, ∀ x:Set, x ∉ E'; PairingAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ x:Set, ∀ y:Set, ∃ P:Set, ∀ z:Set, z ∈ P ⇔ (z = x ∨ z = y)'; UnionAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ F:Set, ∃ U:Set, ∀ x:Set, x ∈ U ⇔ (∃ Y:Set, x ∈ Y ∧ Y ∈ F)'; PowerSetAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ x:Set, ∃ P:Set, ∀ y:Set, y ∈ P ⇔ (y ⊆ x)'; ComprehensionAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ A:Set, ∀ P:Proposition, ∃ B:Set, ∀ x:Set, x ∈ B ⇔ (x ∈ A ∧ P(x))'; ReplacementAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ A:Set, ∀ F:Function, ∃ B:Set, ∀ y:Set, y ∈ B ⇔ (∃ x:Set, x ∈ A ∧ y = F(x))'; InfinityAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∃ I:Set, (∅ ∈ I ∧ ∀ x:Set, x ∈ I → (x ∪ {x}) ∈ I)'; FoundationAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ A:Set, A ≠ ∅ → (∃ x:Set, x ∈ A ∧ x ∩ A = ∅)'; ChoiceAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ F:Set, (∀ A:Set, A ∈ F → A ≠ ∅) → ∃ f:Function, ∀ A:Set, A ∈ F → f(A) ∈ A'; FOL\\_Term ::= Identifier | FunctionSymbol, '(', Term, {',', Term}, ')'; FOL\\_Formula ::= PredicateSymbol, '(', Term, {',', Term}, ')' | '(', FOL\\_Term, '=', FOL\\_Term, ')' | '¬', FOL\\_Formula | '(', FOL\\_Formula, '∧', FOL\\_Formula, ')' | '(', FOL\\_Formula, '∨', FOL\\_Formula, ')' | '(', FOL\\_Formula, '→', FOL\\_Formula, ')' | '∀', Identifier, ',', FOL\\_Formula | '∃', Identifier, ',', FOL\\_Formula; UnivalenceAxiom ::= 'Axiom', '::=', '∀ A:Type, ∀ B:Type, (A ≅ B) ≅ (A = B)'; HigherInductiveType ::= CircleHIT | SphereHIT | SuspensionHIT | QuotientHIT; CircleHIT ::= 'Inductive', 'Circle', ':', 'Type', '::=', '|', 'base', ':', 'Circle', '|', 'loop', ':', 'base = base'; SphereHIT ::= 'Inductive', 'Sphere2', ':', 'Type', '::=', '|', 'base2', ':', 'Sphere2', '|', 'surf2', ':', 'idpath base2 = idpath base2'; SuspensionHIT ::= 'Inductive', 'Susp', '(', 'X:Type', ')', ':', 'Type', '::=', '|', 'north', ':', 'Susp X', '|', 'south', ':', 'Susp X', '|', 'merid', ':', 'X → (north = south)'; QuotientHIT ::= 'Inductive', 'Quotient', '(', 'A:Type', ')', '(', 'R: A → A → Type', ')', ':', 'Type', '::=', '|', 'proj', ':', 'A → Quotient A R', '|', 'relate', ':', 'Π (x y:A), R x y → (proj x = proj y)';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.category-theory-v2.1; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 References: urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 Content-Description: \"The complete, unabridged Category Theory library.\"\n\n  \n\nCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'Category', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'Obj' : Type, 'Hom' : 'Π(a:Obj, b:Obj), Type', 'id' : 'Π(a:Obj), Hom a a', 'compose' : 'Π({a,b,c:Obj}), (Hom b c) -\\\u003e (Hom a b) -\\\u003e (Hom a c)', 'assoc\\_law': 'Π({a,b,c,d:Obj}, f:Hom c d, g:Hom b c, h:Hom a b), compose f (compose g h) = compose (compose f g) h', 'id\\_law\\_L' : 'Π({a,b:Obj}, f:Hom a b), compose (id b) f = f', 'id\\_law\\_R' : 'Π({a,b:Obj}, f:Hom a b), compose f (id a) = f' '}'; FunctorDef ::= 'record', 'Functor', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'Dom' : Category, 'Cod' : Category, 'F\\_Obj' : 'Dom.Obj -\\\u003e Cod.Obj', 'F\\_Map' : 'Π({a,b:Dom.Obj}), (Dom.Hom a b) -\\\u003e (Cod.Hom (F\\_Obj a) (F\\_Obj b))', 'preserves\\_id' : 'Π(a:Dom.Obj), F\\_Map (Dom.id a) = (Cod.id (F\\_Obj a))', 'preserves\\_comp': 'Π({a,b,c:Dom.Obj}, f:Dom.Hom b c, g:Dom.Hom a b), F\\_Map (Dom.compose f g) = Cod.compose (F\\_Map f) (F\\_Map g)' '}'; NatTransDef ::= 'record', 'NaturalTransformation', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'F' : Functor, 'G' : 'Functor where F.Dom=G.Dom and F.Cod=G.Cod', 'alpha' : 'Π(a:F.Dom.Obj), F.Cod.Hom (F.F\\_Obj a) (G.F\\_Obj a)', 'naturality\\_square' : 'Π({a,b:F.Dom.Obj}, f:F.Dom.Hom a b), G.Cod.compose (G.F\\_Map f) (alpha a) = F.Cod.compose (alpha b) (F.F\\_Map f)' '}'; LimitDef ::= 'record', 'Limit', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'L' : ConeDef, 'is\\_terminal': 'Π(C:ConeDef where C.Diagram = L.Diagram), ∃\\!(m : L.Diagram.Cod.Hom C.Tip L.Tip), (C.projection = NatTransDef.compose L.projection (const m))' '}'; ColimitDef ::= 'record', 'Colimit', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'C' : CoconeDef, 'is\\_initial': 'Π(D:CoconeDef where D.Diagram = C.Diagram), ∃\\!(m : C.Diagram.Cod.Hom C.Tip D.Tip), (D.injection = NatTransDef.compose (const m) C.injection)' '}'; AdjointFunctorPair ::= 'record', 'Adjunction', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'F' : Functor, 'G' : 'Functor where F.Dom = G.Cod and F.Cod = G.Dom', 'unit' : 'NatTransDef( (Id F.Dom), (G ∘ F) )', 'counit' : 'NatTransDef( (F ∘ G), (Id F.Cod) )', 'triangle\\_identities': 'Proof( (Π(A:F.Dom.Obj), F.Cod.compose (counit.alpha (F.F\\_Obj A)) (F.F\\_Map (unit.alpha A)) = F.Cod.id (F.F\\_Obj A)) and (Π(B:F.Cod.Obj), G.Dom.compose (G.F\\_Map (counit.alpha B)) (unit.alpha (G.F\\_Obj B)) = G.Dom.id (G.F\\_Obj B)) )' '}'; MonoidalCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'MonoidalCategory', Identifier, 'extends', CategoryDef, '{', 'TensorProduct': 'Functor(Self × Self -\\\u003e Self)', 'UnitObject' : Obj, 'Associator' : 'NatTransDef( ((A ⊗ B) ⊗ C), (A ⊗ (B ⊗ C)) )', 'LeftUnitor' : 'NatTransDef( (UnitObject ⊗ A), A )', 'RightUnitor' : 'NatTransDef( (A ⊗ UnitObject), A )', 'CoherenceLaws': 'Proof( \"The pentagon and triangle identities for the associator and unitors hold.\" )' '}'; EnrichedCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'EnrichedCategory', Identifier, '::=', '{', 'Base' : MonoidalCategoryDef, 'Obj' : Type, 'Hom' : 'Obj -\\\u003e Obj -\\\u003e Base.Obj', 'id' : 'Π(a:Obj), Base.Hom Base.UnitObject (Hom a a)', 'compose' : 'Π({a,b,c:Obj}), Base.Hom ( (Hom b c) ⊗ (Hom a b) ) (Hom a c)', 'axioms' : 'Proof( \"The composition of morphisms is associative and unital, expressed as commutative diagrams in the base monoidal category.\" )' '}'; AbelianCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'AbelianCategory', Identifier, 'extends', CategoryDef, '{', 'IsAdditive' : 'Proof( (Π(a,b:Obj), IsAbelianGroup(Hom a b)) and (Π(f,g:Hom b c, h:Hom a b), (f+g)∘h = f∘h + f∘h) and (Π(f:Hom b c, g,h:Hom a b), f∘(g+h) = f∘g + f∘h) )', 'ZeroObject' : Obj, 'HasBiproducts': 'Proof( Π(A,B:Obj), ∃(C:Obj), IsBiproduct(C,A,B) )', 'HasKernels' : 'Proof( Π(f:Hom a b), ∃(k:Hom (KernelOf f) a), IsKernel(k,f) )', 'HasCokernels' : 'Proof( Π(f:Hom a b), ∃(c:Hom b (CokernelOf f)), IsCokernel(c,f) )', 'MonoIsKernel' : 'Proof( ∀ f:Monomorphism, ∃ g, f = Kernel(g) )', 'EpiIsCokernel' : 'Proof( ∀ f:Epimorphism, ∃ g, f = Cokernel(g) )' '}'; MonadDef ::= 'record', 'Monad', Identifier, 'extends', FunctorDef, '{', 'IsEndofunctor' : 'Proof(Dom ≅ Cod)', 'eta' : 'NatTransDef( (Id Dom), Self )', 'mu' : 'NatTransDef( (Self ∘ Self), Self )', 'monad\\_assoc\\_law': 'Proof( Π(A:Dom.Obj), mu.alpha(A) ∘ Self.F\\_Map(mu.alpha(A)) = mu.alpha(A) ∘ mu.alpha(Self.F\\_Obj(A)) )', 'monad\\_unit\\_law' : 'Proof( (Π(A:Dom.Obj), mu.alpha(A) ∘ Self.F\\_Map(eta.alpha(A)) = id(Self.F\\_Obj(A))) and (Π(A:Dom.Obj), mu.alpha(A) ∘ eta.alpha(Self.F\\_Obj(A)) = id(Self.F\\_Obj(A))) )' '}'; ElementaryToposDef ::= 'record', 'ElementaryTopos', Identifier, 'extends', CategoryDef, '{', 'IsCartesianClosed': 'Proof( HasTerminalObject and HasAllBinaryProducts and HasAllExponentialObjects )', 'HasTerminalObject': 'Proof( ∃(T:Obj), ∀(A:Obj), ∃\\!(f:A-\\\u003eT) )', 'SubobjectClassifier': Obj, 'TruthMorphism' : 'Hom TerminalObject SubobjectClassifier', 'PowerObject' : 'Π(A:Obj), Obj', 'ClassifierAxiom' : 'Proof( Π(m:Monomorphism from S to X), ∃\\!(chi\\_m : Hom X SubobjectClassifier), (IsPullback(S, TerminalObject, m, chi\\_m)) )', 'PowerObjectAxiom' : 'Proof( Π(A:Obj), Isomorphism(Hom(B, PowerObject(A)), Subobjects(B × A)) )' '}'; NaturalNumberObjectDef ::= 'record', 'NaturalNumberObject', Identifier, 'extends', Obj, '{', 'Zero': 'Hom TerminalObject Self', 'Succ': 'Hom Self Self', 'UniversalProperty': 'Proof( Π(X:Obj, q:Hom TerminalObject X, f:Hom X X), ∃\\!(u:Hom Self X), (u ∘ Zero = q) and (u ∘ Succ = f ∘ u) )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.control-theory-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-control-theory-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-dynamical-systems-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-numerical-analysis-v2.19 Content-Description: \"Comprehensive Control Theory: Optimal, Robust, Adaptive, Nonlinear, Hybrid, Networked, and Model Predictive Control.\"\n\n  \n\nControlSystemDef ::= 'record', 'ControlSystem', Identifier, '{', 'Plant' : 'Rule( (Input, State), \"=\\\u003e\", State )', 'Controller' : 'Rule( (Reference, State), \"=\\\u003e\", Input )', 'ClosedLoopSystem' : 'Rule( (Reference, InitialState), \"=\\\u003e\", StateTrajectory )', 'StabilityProperty' : 'Proof( \"Closed-loop system is stable\" )', 'PerformanceMetric' : 'Rule( StateTrajectory, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )' '}'; LinearTimeInvariantDef ::= 'record', 'LinearTimeInvariant', Identifier, '{', 'StateMatrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'InputMatrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'OutputMatrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'FeedthroughMatrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'TransferFunction' : 'Rule( ComplexDef, \"=\\\u003e\", Matrix(ComplexDef) )', 'ControllabilityProperty' : 'Proof( \"System is controllable\" )' '}'; OptimalControlDef ::= 'record', 'OptimalControl', Identifier, '{', 'CostFunctional' : 'Rule( (StateTrajectory, ControlTrajectory), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'HamiltonianFunction' : 'Rule( (State, Costate, Control, Time), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'PontryaginMaximumPrinciple' : 'Proof( \"Necessary conditions for optimality\" )', 'BellmanEquation' : 'Dynamic programming formulation', 'RiccatiEquation' : 'Matrix differential equation for LQR' '}'; RobustControlDef ::= 'record', 'RobustControl', Identifier, '{', 'UncertaintyModel' : 'Set of possible plant models', 'H\\_InfinityNorm' : 'Rule( TransferFunction, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'SmallGainTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Stability condition for feedback systems\" )', 'μSynthesis' : 'Design methodology for structured uncertainty', 'LinearMatrixInequality' : 'Convex optimization formulation' '}'; AdaptiveControlDef ::= 'record', 'AdaptiveControl', Identifier, '{', 'ParameterEstimation' : 'Rule( (InputOutput\\_Data, Time), \"=\\\u003e\", ParameterVector )', 'AdaptationLaw' : 'Rule( (TrackingError, RegressionVector), \"=\\\u003e\", ParameterUpdate )', 'LyapunovStability' : 'Proof( \"Adaptive system maintains stability\" )', 'PersistentExcitation' : 'Condition for parameter convergence', 'ModelReferenceAdaptive' : 'Tracking a reference model' '}'; NonlinearControlDef ::= 'record', 'NonlinearControl', Identifier, '{', 'LyapunovFunction' : 'Rule( State, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'LieDerivative' : 'Directional derivative along vector field', 'ControlLyapunovFunction' : 'Lyapunov function for control design', 'FeedbackLinearization' : 'Transformation to linear form via feedback', 'SlidingModeControl' : 'Discontinuous control for robustness' '}'; ModelPredictiveControlDef ::= 'record', 'ModelPredictiveControl', Identifier, '{', 'PredictionHorizon' : Nat, 'ControlHorizon' : Nat, 'OptimizationProblem' : 'Rule( (CurrentState, PredictionModel), \"=\\\u003e\", OptimalControlSequence )', 'ReceedingHorizonPrinciple' : 'Implementation strategy', 'ConstraintHandling' : 'Method for input/output constraints' '}'; HybridControlDef ::= 'record', 'HybridControl', Identifier, '{', 'DiscreteState' : 'Finite set of modes', 'ContinuousState' : VectorSpaceDef, 'SwitchingLogic' : 'Rule( (DiscreteState, ContinuousState), \"=\\\u003e\", DiscreteState )', 'GuardConditions' : 'Conditions for mode transitions', 'ResetMaps' : 'State updates at switching instants' '}'; NetworkedControlDef ::= 'record', 'NetworkedControl', Identifier, '{', 'CommunicationNetwork' : NetworkTopologyDef, 'PacketDropout' : 'Probabilistic model of communication failures', 'TimeDelay' : 'Delay in information transmission', 'QuantizationEffects' : 'Digital implementation constraints', 'ConsensusProtocol' : 'Distributed agreement algorithm' '}'; StochasticControlDef ::= 'record', 'StochasticControl', Identifier, '{', 'StochasticDifferentialEquation' : 'SDE with control input', 'BellmanEquation' : 'HJB equation for optimal control', 'KalmanFilter' : 'Optimal state estimation', 'SeparationPrinciple' : 'Independence of estimation and control', 'RiskSensitiveControl' : 'Control with risk considerations' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.dynamical-systems-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-dynamical-systems-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-analysis-advanced-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 Content-Description: \"Comprehensive Dynamical Systems and Chaos Theory: Ergodic Theory, Stability Theory, Attractors, Bifurcation Theory, Non-linear Dynamics.\"\n\n  \n\nDynamicalSystemDef ::= 'record', 'DynamicalSystem', Identifier, '{', 'StateSpace' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'TimeSpace' : 'RealDef | IntegerDef', 'EvolutionMap' : 'Rule( (State, Time), \"=\\\u003e\", State )', 'FlowProperty' : 'Proof( \"φ(t+s,x) = φ(t,φ(s,x))\" )', 'ContinuityProperty' : 'Proof( \"Evolution map is continuous\" )' '}'; FixedPointDef ::= 'record', 'FixedPoint', Identifier, '{', 'Point' : 'Element of StateSpace', 'FixedPointProperty' : 'Proof( \"f(x) = x\" )', 'StabilityType' : 'Stable | Unstable | SaddleType', 'LinearizationMatrix' : 'Jacobian at fixed point', 'StabilityAnalysis' : 'Eigenvalue analysis for stability' '}'; PeriodicOrbitDef ::= 'record', 'PeriodicOrbit', Identifier, '{', 'OrbitPoints' : 'Set(StateSpace)', 'Period' : RealDef, 'PeriodicProperty' : 'Proof( \"φ(T,x) = x for all x in orbit\" )', 'PoincaréMap' : 'First return map to cross-section', 'FloguetMultipliers' : 'Eigenvalues of linearized Poincaré map' '}'; AttractorDef ::= 'record', 'Attractor', Identifier, '{', 'AttractingSet' : 'Compact invariant set', 'BasinOfAttraction' : 'Set of initial conditions converging to attractor', 'AttractorType' : 'FixedPoint | LimitCycle | ChaoticAttractor', 'LyapunovExponents' : 'Rates of exponential divergence', 'Dimension' : 'Hausdorff or box-counting dimension' '}'; ChaosDef ::= 'record', 'Chaos', Identifier, '{', 'SensitiveDependence' : 'Proof( \"Small changes in initial conditions lead to large differences\" )', 'TopologicalMixing' : 'Proof( \"System is topologically mixing\" )', 'DensePeriodicOrbits' : 'Proof( \"Periodic orbits are dense\" )', 'LyapunovExponent' : 'Positive largest Lyapunov exponent', 'FractalStructure' : 'Strange attractor has fractal geometry' '}'; BifurcationTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'BifurcationTheory', Identifier, '{', 'ParameterSpace' : VectorSpaceDef, 'BifurcationParameter' : 'Element of ParameterSpace', 'BifurcationPoint' : 'Parameter value where qualitative change occurs', 'BifurcationType' : 'SaddleNode | Transcritical | Pitchfork | Hopf', 'NormalForm' : 'Simplified form near bifurcation point' '}'; ErgodicTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'ErgodicTheory', Identifier, '{', 'MeasurePreservingTransformation' : 'Rule( StateSpace, \"=\\\u003e\", StateSpace )', 'InvariantMeasure' : 'Measure preserved by transformation', 'ErgodicProperty' : 'Proof( \"Time averages equal space averages\" )', 'MixingProperty' : 'Statistical independence in the limit', 'BirkhoffErgodicTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Convergence of time averages\" )' '}'; HamiltonianDynamicsDef ::= 'record', 'HamiltonianDynamics', Identifier, '{', 'PhaseSpace' : 'Cotangent bundle of configuration space', 'HamiltonianFunction' : 'Rule( PhaseSpace, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'SymplecticStructure' : 'Non-degenerate closed 2-form', 'HamiltonsEquations' : 'Canonical equations of motion', 'CanonicalTransformation' : 'Symplectic diffeomorphism' '}'; IntegrabilitDef ::= 'record', 'Integrability', Identifier, '{', 'FirstIntegals' : 'Conserved quantities in involution', 'LiouvilleTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Integrable systems are solvable by quadratures\" )', 'ActionAngleVariables' : 'Canonical coordinates for integrable systems', 'KAMTheory' : 'Persistence of quasi-periodic motion', 'ArnoldDiffusion' : 'Slow drift in near-integrable systems' '}'; StabilityTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'StabilityTheory', Identifier, '{', 'LyapunovStability' : 'Stability in the sense of Lyapunov', 'AsymptoticStability' : 'Stability with convergence to equilibrium', 'LyapunovFunction' : 'Scalar function for stability analysis', 'LinearStabilityAnalysis' : 'Stability via linearization', 'ConverseLyapunovTheorem' : 'Existence of Lyapunov functions' '}'; TurbulenceDef ::= 'record', 'Turbulence', Identifier, '{', 'NavierStokesEquation' : 'PDE governing fluid motion', 'ReynoldsNumber' : 'Dimensionless parameter', 'TurbulentCascade' : 'Energy transfer across scales', 'Kolmogorov41Theory' : 'Statistical theory of turbulence', 'StrangeAttractor' : 'Chaotic attractor in turbulent flow' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.applied-statistics-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-applied-statistics-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-probability-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-numerical-analysis-v2.19 Content-Description: \"Comprehensive Applied Statistics: Statistical Inference, Regression Analysis, Time Series Analysis, Machine Learning Fundamentals.\"\n\n  \n\nStatisticalModelDef ::= 'record', 'StatisticalModel', Identifier, '{', 'ParameterSpace' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'SampleSpace' : MeasurableSpaceDef, 'ParametricFamily' : 'Rule( ParameterSpace, \"=\\\u003e\", ProbabilityMeasureDef )', 'Likelihood' : 'Rule( (Parameter, Data), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'Identifiability' : 'Proof( \"Different parameters yield different distributions\" )' '}'; EstimationTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'EstimationTheory', Identifier, '{', 'Estimator' : 'Rule( SampleData, \"=\\\u003e\", ParameterEstimate )', 'Bias' : 'Rule( Estimator, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'Variance' : 'Rule( Estimator, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'MeanSquaredError' : 'Bias² + Variance', 'CramerRaoLowerBound' : 'Lower bound on estimator variance', 'MaximumLikelihoodEstimator' : 'Estimator maximizing likelihood function' '}'; HypothesisTestingDef ::= 'record', 'HypothesisTesting', Identifier, '{', 'NullHypothesis' : 'Statistical hypothesis to be tested', 'AlternativeHypothesis' : 'Competing hypothesis', 'TestStatistic' : 'Rule( SampleData, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'CriticalRegion' : 'Region for rejecting null hypothesis', 'TypeIError' : 'Probability of falsely rejecting null', 'TypeIIError' : 'Probability of falsely accepting null' '}'; RegressionAnalysisDef ::= 'record', 'RegressionAnalysis', Identifier, '{', 'ResponseVariable' : 'Dependent variable', 'ExplanatoryVariables' : 'Independent variables', 'RegressionFunction' : 'Rule( ExplanatoryVariables, \"=\\\u003e\", ExpectedResponse )', 'LinearRegression' : 'Linear relationship between variables', 'NonlinearRegression' : 'Nonlinear relationship', 'ResidualAnalysis' : 'Analysis of model fit' '}'; TimeSeriesAnalysisDef ::= 'record', 'TimeSeriesAnalysis', Identifier, '{', 'TimeSeries' : 'Sequence of observations over time', 'StationarityProperty' : 'Statistical properties constant over time', 'AutocorrelationFunction' : 'Correlation of series with itself at different lags', 'ARIMA\\_Model' : 'Autoregressive integrated moving average model', 'SpectralDensity' : 'Frequency domain representation', 'Forecasting' : 'Prediction of future values' '}'; MultivariateMethods ::= 'record', 'MultivariateStatistics', Identifier, '{', 'MultivariateNormalDistribution' : 'Joint normal distribution', 'PrincipalComponentAnalysis' : 'Dimensionality reduction technique', 'CanonicalCorrelationAnalysis' : 'Relationship between variable sets', 'FactorAnalysis' : 'Latent variable model', 'ClusterAnalysis' : 'Grouping of observations', 'DiscriminantAnalysis' : 'Classification method' '}'; ProofObjectHandle ::= 'handle', '\\\u003c', Term, '\\\u003e'; KernelProver ::= 'Kernel.Prover'; DirectedTypeTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'DirectedTypeTheory', Identifier, 'extends', 'HoTT', '{', 'DirectedPath' : 'Π (a:Type, b:Type), Type', 'id\\_dir' : 'Π (a:Type), DirectedPath a a', 'compose\\_dir' : 'Π ({a,b,c:Type}), (DirectedPath b c) -\\\u003e (DirectedPath a b) -\\\u003e (DirectedPath a c)', 'assoc\\_law\\_dir': 'Proof( Π({a,b,c,d:Type}, p:(DirectedPath c d), q:(DirectedPath b c), r:(DirectedPath a b)), (compose\\_dir p (compose\\_dir q r)) = (compose\\_dir (compose\\_dir p q) r) )', 'id\\_laws\\_dir' : 'Proof( Π({a,b:Type}, p:(DirectedPath a b)), ((compose\\_dir (id\\_dir b) p) = p) ∧ ((compose\\_dir p (id\\_dir a)) = p) )' }'; DirectedPath ::= 'path', Term, '→', Term; OrthoPath ::= 'ortho', '(', DirectedPath, ',', DirectedPath, ')'; SyncHomotopy ::= 'homotopy', '(', DirectedPath, '⇒', DirectedPath, ')'; LinearType ::= 'linear', '(', Type, ')'; ResourceTerm ::= 'resource', '(', Term, ':', LinearType, ')'; IPC\\_Channel ::= 'ipc\\_channel', '(', 'Process', ',', 'Process', ',', ResourceTerm, ')'; DependentSessionType ::= 'record', 'DependentSessionType', Identifier, '{', 'Protocol' : ProtocolDef, 'Channel' : ChannelType, 'StateDependentMessage' : 'Π(s:Type), Type', 'EnsuresProgress' : 'Proof( \"The protocol guarantees termination or non-blocking progress\" )', 'EnsuresTypeSafety' : 'Proof( \"Messages adhere to expected types at each protocol step\" )' '}'; HomotopicalConcurrencyMonad ::= 'record', 'HomotopicalConcurrencyMonad', Identifier, 'extends', MonadDef, '{', 'Computations' : Type, 'Unit' : Type, 'Bind' : 'Π(c:Computations, f: (Type -\\\u003e Computations)), Type', 'PathComposition' : 'Rule( (c1:Computations, c2:Computations, p:PathHomotopyEquivalenceProof), \"=\\\u003e\", Computations)', 'ConcurrencyAxioms' : 'Proof( \"Higher-dimensional consistency axioms for concurrent operations\" )' '}'; ProofCarryingCodeDef ::= 'record', 'ProofCarryingCode', Identifier, '{', 'Code' : Term, 'SafetyPredicate' : Proposition, 'Proof' : ProofObjectHandle, 'VerificationEngine' : FormalVerificationToolDef, 'TrustAnchor' : 'SignedCodeCertificateDef | HardwareRootOfTrustDef' '}'; CategoricalResourceAlgebraDef ::= 'record', 'CategoricalResourceAlgebra', Identifier, 'extends', MonoidalCategoryDef, '{', 'Objects' : ResourceTerm, 'Morphisms' : Rule, 'TensorProduct' : 'Functor(Self × Self -\\\u003e Self)', 'LinearityAxioms' : 'Proof( \"Axioms ensuring precise, linear consumption and production of resources\" )', 'IsResourceInvariant' : 'Rule( (R:ResourceTerm, T:Rule, Algebra:Self), \"=\\\u003e\", boolean )' '}'; LivePatchingFormalismDef ::= 'record', 'LivePatchingFormalism', Identifier, '{', 'OldModule' : HotSwapModuleDef, 'NewModule' : HotSwapModuleDef, 'Delta' : Term, 'ConsistencyPreservationProof' : 'Proof( \"Proof that the patch maintains system invariants and coherence\" )', 'RollbackMechanism' : 'Rule(Self, \"=\\\u003e\", SystemCall)' '}'; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-meta-architecture-v2.11 References: urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 urn:uuid:lib-hott-os-v2.19 Content-Description: \"Updated: Meta-Architectural Primitives, now including Higher-Categorical Model Synthesis and Proof Refinement, and Automated Discovery.\"\n\n  \n\nMeta\\_ArchitecturalPrimitive ::= 'MetaArchitecture' | 'RingStarDuality' | 'ZeroElisionsPrinciple' | 'ModularInterpretation' | 'AtomicReification' | 'CycleCenterConcept'; Meta\\_PushoutConstruction ::= 'record', 'PushoutConstruction', Identifier, '{', 'BaseSystem' : Type, 'Extension1' : Type, 'Extension2' : Type, 'UnificationMorphism' : 'Rule( (Extension1, Extension2), \"=\\\u003e\", UnifiedSystem )', 'UniversalProperty' : 'Proof( \"Pushout satisfies universal property\" )' '}'; AutomatedSpecificationGenerator ::= 'record', 'AutomatedSpecificationGenerator', Identifier, '{', 'InputRequirements' : 'Set(Proposition)', 'GenerationEngine' : 'Rule( InputRequirements, \"=\\\u003e\", FormalSpecification )', 'CorrectnessProof' : 'Proof( \"Generated specification satisfies input requirements\" )', 'OptimalityMetric' : 'Rule( FormalSpecification, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )' '}'; ProofRefinementEngine ::= 'record', 'ProofRefinementEngine', Identifier, '{', 'InitialProof' : ProofObjectHandle, 'RefinementStrategy' : 'Rule( ProofObjectHandle, \"=\\\u003e\", RefinedProof )', 'OptimizationCriteria' : 'Set(Proposition)', 'RefinementCorrectness' : 'Proof( \"Refined proof maintains validity while improving efficiency\" )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.epistemic-topology-v2.16; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-topology-v2.16 References: urn:uuid:lib-topos-theory-v2.8 urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 Content-Description: \"Updated: Epistemic Topology and Capabilities, now with Embodied Cognition.\"\n\n  \n\nEpistemicTopologyDef ::= 'record', 'EpistemicTopology', Identifier, 'extends', GrothendieckToposDef, '{', 'KnowledgeSpace' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'BeliefStructure' : SheafOnLocaleDef, 'EpistemicModality' : 'Rule( Proposition, \"=\\\u003e\", EpistemicJudgement )', 'KnowledgeEvolution' : 'Rule( (EpistemicState, Evidence), \"=\\\u003e\", UpdatedEpistemicState )' '}'; PerceptionBoundaryDef ::= 'record', 'PerceptionBoundary', Identifier, '{', 'PerceptualSpace' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'BoundaryConditions' : 'Set(Proposition)', 'AccessibilityFunction' : 'Rule( (Observer, Phenomenon), \"=\\\u003e\", AccessibilityDegree )', 'AssociatedContext' : GrothendieckToposDef '}'; SharedPerceptionBoundaryDef ::= 'record', 'SharedPerceptionBoundary', Identifier, 'extends', PerceptionBoundaryDef, '{', 'Observers' : 'Set(Observer)', 'ConsensusFunction' : 'Rule( Set(Observation), \"=\\\u003e\", ConsensusReality )', 'SharedContext' : GrothendieckToposDef, 'IntersubjectivityProof' : 'Proof( \"Shared perceptions maintain coherence across observers\" )' '}'; PerceptualDiscrepancyDef ::= 'record', 'PerceptualDiscrepancy', Identifier, '{', 'Observer1' : Observer, 'Observer2' : Observer, 'Phenomenon' : Type, 'DiscrepancyMeasure' : 'Rule( (Observation, Observation), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'ResolutionStrategy' : BridgingMechanismDef '}'; BridgingMechanismDef ::= 'record', 'BridgingMechanism', Identifier, '{', 'SourceContext' : GrothendieckToposDef, 'TargetContext' : GrothendieckToposDef, 'BridgingMorphism' : GeometricMorphismDef, 'TranslationFunction' : 'Rule( (Knowledge\\_in\\_SourceContext), \"=\\\u003e\", Knowledge\\_in\\_TargetContext )', 'PreservationProof' : 'Proof( \"Essential structure preserved across contexts\" )' '}'; EmbodiedAgentDef ::= 'record', 'EmbodiedAgent', Identifier, '{', 'PhysicalBody' : SmoothManifoldDef, 'SensorySystems' : 'Set(SensorDef)', 'ActuationSystems' : 'Set(ActuatorDef)', 'EnvironmentalModel' : 'Rule( SensoryInput, \"=\\\u003e\", EnvironmentalRepresentation )', 'ActuationPolicy' : 'Rule( (EnvironmentalRepresentation, Goal), \"=\\\u003e\", MotorCommand )' '}'; PerceptualObjectDef ::= 'record', 'PerceptualObject', Identifier, '{', 'SpatialLocation' : 'Point in SmoothManifoldDef', 'TemporalExtent' : TemporalFrameDef, 'Affordances' : 'Set(AffordanceDef)', 'PerceptualProperties' : 'Map(PropertyType, PropertyValue)' '}'; EmbodiedCognitionDef ::= 'record', 'EmbodiedCognition', Identifier, '{', 'Agent' : EmbodiedAgentDef, 'CognitiveState' : 'Rule( (SensoryInput, MotorState), \"=\\\u003e\", MentalRepresentation )', 'SensoriomotorLoop' : 'Rule( (Perception, Action), \"=\\\u003e\", (UpdatedPerception, NextAction) )', 'EmbodiedInference' : 'Rule( (EmbodiedExperience), \"=\\\u003e\", AbstractConcept )' '}'; StochasticProcessDef ::= 'record', 'StochasticProcess', Identifier, '{', 'ProbabilitySpace' : ProbabilitySpaceDef, 'StateSpace' : MeasurableSpaceDef, 'ProcessFunction' : 'Rule( (Time, RandomOutcome), \"=\\\u003e\", State )', 'FiltrationStructure' : 'Sequence(SigmaAlgebra)' '}'; MartingaleDef ::= 'record', 'Martingale', Identifier, 'extends', StochasticProcessDef, '{', 'MartingaleProperty' : 'Proof( \"E\\[X\\_t | F\\_s\\] = X\\_s for all t \\\u003e s\" )', 'IntegrabilityCondition' : 'Proof( \"All random variables are integrable\" )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.numerical-analysis-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-numerical-analysis-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-number-systems-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 Content-Description: \"Formalization of Numerical Analysis: Root Finding, Optimization, Numerical Integration, ODEs, PDEs, Linear Algebra.\"\n\n  \n\nNewtonsMethodDef ::= 'record', 'NewtonsMethod', Identifier, '{', 'Function' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e RealDef', 'Derivative' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e RealDef', 'InitialGuess' : RealDef, 'IterationFormula' : 'Rule( (x\\_n:RealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (x\\_n - Function(x\\_n)/Derivative(x\\_n)) )', 'ConvergenceProof' : 'Proof( \"Method converges quadratically under appropriate conditions\" )' '}'; BisectionMethodDef ::= 'record', 'BisectionMethod', Identifier, '{', 'Function' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e RealDef', 'Interval' : '(RealDef, RealDef)', 'Tolerance' : RealDef, 'BisectionRule' : 'Rule( (a:RealDef, b:RealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", RootApproximation )', 'ConvergenceGuarantee' : 'Proof( \"Linear convergence guaranteed\" )' '}'; GradientDescentDef ::= 'record', 'GradientDescent', Identifier, '{', 'ObjectiveFunction' : 'Vector(RealDef) -\\\u003e RealDef', 'Gradient' : 'Vector(RealDef) -\\\u003e Vector(RealDef)', 'LearningRate' : RealDef, 'UpdateRule' : 'Rule( (x\\_n:Vector(RealDef)), \"=\\\u003e\", (x\\_n - LearningRate \\* Gradient(x\\_n)) )', 'ConvergenceAnalysis' : 'Proof( \"Convergence conditions and rates\" )' '}'; TrapezoidalRuleDef ::= 'record', 'TrapezoidalRule', Identifier, '{', 'Function' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e RealDef', 'Interval' : '(RealDef, RealDef)', 'Subdivisions' : 'Nat', 'ApproximationFormula' : 'Rule( (f, \\[a,b\\], n), \"=\\\u003e\", SumApproximation )', 'ErrorBound' : 'Proof( \"Error is O(h²) where h is step size\" )' '}'; SimpsonsRuleDef ::= 'record', 'SimpsonsRule', Identifier, '{', 'Function' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e RealDef', 'Interval' : '(RealDef, RealDef)', 'Subdivisions' : 'EvenNat', 'ApproximationFormula' : 'Rule( (f, \\[a,b\\], n), \"=\\\u003e\", WeightedSumApproximation )', 'ErrorBound' : 'Proof( \"Error is O(h⁴) where h is step size\" )' '}'; EulersMethodDef ::= 'record', 'EulersMethod', Identifier, '{', 'DifferentialEquation' : 'Rule( (t:RealDef, y:RealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'InitialCondition' : '(RealDef, RealDef)', 'StepSize' : RealDef, 'IterationFormula' : 'Rule( (t\\_n, y\\_n), \"=\\\u003e\", (t\\_n + h, y\\_n + h\\*f(t\\_n, y\\_n)) )', 'LocalTruncationError' : 'Proof( \"Local error is O(h²)\" )' '}'; RungeKuttaMethodDef ::= 'record', 'RungeKuttaMethod', Identifier, '{', 'DifferentialEquation' : 'Rule( (t:RealDef, y:RealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'InitialCondition' : '(RealDef, RealDef)', 'StepSize' : RealDef, 'WeightedAverageFormula' : 'Rule( (k1, k2, k3, k4), \"=\\\u003e\", WeightedCombination )', 'AccuracyOrder' : 'Proof( \"Fourth-order accuracy\" )' '}'; FiniteDifferenceMethodDef ::= 'record', 'FiniteDifferenceMethod', Identifier, '{', 'PartialDifferentialEquation' : 'Rule( (SpatialVariable, TemporalVariable, Solution), \"=\\\u003e\", Equation )', 'Grid' : GridDef, 'DiscretizationScheme' : 'Rule( (ContinuousOperator), \"=\\\u003e\", DiscreteOperator )', 'StabilityAnalysis' : 'Proof( \"Numerical stability conditions\" )' '}'; GaussianEliminationDef ::= 'record', 'GaussianElimination', Identifier, '{', 'Matrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'Vector' : 'Vector(RealDef)', 'ForwardElimination' : 'Rule( Matrix, \"=\\\u003e\", UpperTriangularMatrix )', 'BackSubstitution' : 'Rule( (UpperTriangularMatrix, Vector), \"=\\\u003e\", Solution )', 'ComplexityAnalysis' : 'Proof( \"O(n³) operations\" )' '}'; LUDecompositionDef ::= 'record', 'LUDecomposition', Identifier, '{', 'Matrix' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'LowerTriangular' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'UpperTriangular' : 'Matrix(RealDef)', 'DecompositionProperty' : 'Proof( \"A = LU\" )', 'EfficiencyProof' : 'Proof( \"Efficient for multiple right-hand sides\" )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.computational-geometry-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-computational-geometry-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-combinatorics-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-number-systems-v2.3 Content-Description: \"Formalization of Computational Geometry: Algorithms for geometric objects, their properties, and spatial relationships.\"\n\n  \n\nPointDef ::= 'record', 'Point', Identifier, '{', 'Coordinates' : 'Vector(RealDef)', 'Dimension' : Nat, 'DistanceFunction' : 'Rule( (Point, Point), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )' '}'; LineSegmentDef ::= 'record', 'LineSegment', Identifier, '{', 'StartPoint' : PointDef, 'EndPoint' : PointDef, 'Length' : RealDef, 'ParametricRepresentation' : 'Rule( (t:RealDef where 0 ≤ t ≤ 1), \"=\\\u003e\", PointDef )' '}'; VectorDef ::= 'record', 'Vector', Identifier, '{', 'Components' : 'Sequence(RealDef)', 'Dimension' : Nat, 'Magnitude' : RealDef, 'DotProduct' : 'Rule( (Vector, Vector), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'CrossProduct' : 'Rule( (Vector, Vector), \"=\\\u003e\", Vector )' '}'; PolygonDef ::= 'record', 'Polygon', Identifier, '{', 'Vertices' : 'Sequence(PointDef)', 'Edges' : 'Sequence(LineSegmentDef)', 'Area' : RealDef, 'Perimeter' : RealDef, 'IsConvex' : 'Proof( \"All interior angles less than 180 degrees\" )' '}'; ConvexHullDef ::= 'record', 'ConvexHull', Identifier, '{', 'InputPoints' : 'Set(PointDef)', 'HullVertices' : 'Sequence(PointDef)', 'GrahamscanAlgorithm' : 'Rule( Set(PointDef), \"=\\\u003e\", ConvexPolygon )', 'OptimalityProof' : 'Proof( \"Smallest convex set containing all input points\" )' '}'; VoronoiDiagramDef ::= 'record', 'VoronoiDiagram', Identifier, '{', 'Sites' : 'Set(PointDef)', 'VoronoiCells' : 'Set(ConvexPolygon)', 'DelaunayDual' : DelaunayTriangulationDef, 'ConstructionAlgorithm' : 'Rule( Set(PointDef), \"=\\\u003e\", VoronoiDiagram )' '}'; DelaunayTriangulationDef ::= 'record', 'DelaunayTriangulation', Identifier, '{', 'Points' : 'Set(PointDef)', 'Triangles' : 'Set(Triangle)', 'DelaunayProperty' : 'Proof( \"No point lies inside circumcircle of any triangle\" )', 'MaximizesMinAngle' : 'Proof( \"Maximizes minimum angle among all triangulations\" )' '}'; OrientationTestDef ::= 'record', 'OrientationTest', Identifier, '{', 'Points' : '(PointDef, PointDef, PointDef)', 'OrientationFunction' : 'Rule( (p, q, r), \"=\\\u003e\", (Clockwise | CounterClockwise | Collinear) )', 'DeterminantFormula' : 'Proof( \"Based on sign of determinant\" )' '}'; PointInPolygonTestDef ::= 'record', 'PointInPolygonTest', Identifier, '{', 'Point' : PointDef, 'Polygon' : PolygonDef, 'RaycastingAlgorithm' : 'Rule( (Point, Polygon), \"=\\\u003e\", (Inside | Outside | OnBoundary) )', 'ComplexityAnalysis' : 'Proof( \"O(n) time complexity\" )' '}'; LineSegmentIntersectionDef ::= 'record', 'LineSegmentIntersection', Identifier, '{', 'Segment1' : LineSegmentDef, 'Segment2' : LineSegmentDef, 'IntersectionTest' : 'Rule( (LineSegment, LineSegment), \"=\\\u003e\", (Intersect | Parallel | Skew) )', 'IntersectionPoint' : 'Option(PointDef)' '}'; ClosestPairProblemDef ::= 'record', 'ClosestPairProblem', Identifier, '{', 'Points' : 'Set(PointDef)', 'DivideAndConquerAlgorithm' : 'Rule( Set(PointDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (PointDef, PointDef) )', 'TimeComplexity' : 'Proof( \"O(n log n) time complexity\" )', 'DistanceFunction' : 'Rule( (PointDef, PointDef), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )' '}'; GridDef ::= 'record', 'Grid', Identifier, '{', 'Dimensions' : 'Vector(Nat)', 'CellSize' : RealDef, 'IndexingFunction' : 'Rule( Coordinates, \"=\\\u003e\", GridCell )', 'NeighborhoodStructure' : 'Rule( GridCell, \"=\\\u003e\", Set(GridCell) )' '}'; MeshDef ::= 'record', 'Mesh', Identifier, '{', 'Vertices' : 'Set(PointDef)', 'Elements' : 'Set(GeometricElement)', 'Connectivity' : GraphDef, 'QualityMetrics' : 'Set(RealDef)' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.number-theory-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-number-theory-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-number-systems-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 Content-Description: \"Formalization of Number Theory: Divisibility, primes, modular arithmetic, and foundations of algebraic and analytic number theory.\"\n\n  \n\nDivisibilityDef ::= 'record', 'Divisibility', Identifier, '{', 'Dividend' : IntegerDef, 'Divisor' : IntegerDef, 'DivisibilityRelation' : 'Rule( (a:IntegerDef, b:IntegerDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (b | a ⟺ ∃k:IntegerDef, a = k*b) )' '}'; PrimeNumberDef ::= 'record', 'PrimeNumber', Identifier, '{', 'Number' : IntegerDef, 'PrimalityTest' : 'Rule( p:IntegerDef, \"=\\\u003e\", (IsPrime(p) ⟺ (p \\\u003e 1 ∧ ∀d:IntegerDef, (d | p → d = 1 ∨ d = p))) )', 'FundamentalTheoremConnection' : 'Proof( \"Every integer has unique prime factorization\" )' '}'; ModularArithmeticDef ::= 'record', 'ModularArithmetic', Identifier, '{', 'Modulus' : IntegerDef, 'CongruenceRelation' : 'Rule( (a, b, m), \"=\\\u003e\", (a ≡ b (mod m) ⟺ m | (a - b)) )', 'ModularAddition' : 'Rule( (a, b, m), \"=\\\u003e\", ((a + b) mod m) )', 'ModularMultiplication' : 'Rule( (a, b, m), \"=\\\u003e\", ((a \\* b) mod m) )' '}'; EulersTotientFunctionDef ::= 'record', 'EulersTotientFunction', Identifier, '{', 'Input' : IntegerDef, 'TotientValue' : 'Rule( n:IntegerDef, \"=\\\u003e\", |{k:IntegerDef | 1 ≤ k ≤ n ∧ gcd(k,n) = 1}| )', 'MultiplicativeProperty' : 'Proof( \"φ(mn) = φ(m)φ(n) when gcd(m,n) = 1\" )', 'EulersTheorem' : 'Proof( \"If gcd(a,n) = 1, then a^φ(n) ≡ 1 (mod n)\" )' '}'; ChineseRemainderTheoremDef ::= 'record', 'ChineseRemainderTheorem', Identifier, '{', 'ModuliSystem' : 'Sequence(IntegerDef)', 'RemaindersSystem' : 'Sequence(IntegerDef)', 'UniqueSolution' : 'Proof( \"System has unique solution modulo product of moduli when moduli are pairwise coprime\" )', 'ConstructiveAlgorithm' : 'Rule( (ModuliSystem, RemaindersSystem), \"=\\\u003e\", Solution )' '}'; FermatsLittleTheoremDef ::= 'record', 'FermatsLittleTheorem', Identifier, '{', 'Prime' : PrimeNumberDef, 'Base' : IntegerDef, 'Statement' : 'Proof( \"If p is prime and p ∤ a, then a^(p-1) ≡ 1 (mod p)\" )', 'Generalization' : EulersTotientFunctionDef '}'; QuadraticResiduesDef ::= 'record', 'QuadraticResidues', Identifier, '{', 'Modulus' : PrimeNumberDef, 'Residue' : IntegerDef, 'LegendreSymbol' : 'Rule( (a, p), \"=\\\u003e\", (a/p) )', 'QuadraticReciprocityLaw' : 'Proof( \"Fundamental reciprocity relation for Legendre symbols\" )' '}'; PellsEquationDef ::= 'record', 'PellsEquation', Identifier, '{', 'NonSquareInteger' : IntegerDef, 'Equation' : 'x² - D*y² = 1', 'FundamentalSolution' : '(IntegerDef, IntegerDef)', 'ContinuedFractionConnection' : 'Proof( \"Solutions related to continued fraction expansion of √D\" )' '}'; DirichletCharacterDef ::= 'record', 'DirichletCharacter', Identifier, '{', 'Modulus' : IntegerDef, 'CharacterFunction' : 'Rule( IntegerDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'MultiplicativeProperty' : 'Proof( \"χ(mn) = χ(m)χ(n)\" )', 'PeriodicityProperty' : 'Proof( \"χ(n + k) = χ(n) for all n\" )' '}'; ArithmeticFunctionDef ::= 'record', 'ArithmeticFunction', Identifier, '{', 'Domain' : IntegerDef, 'Codomain' : ComplexDef, 'FunctionValue' : 'Rule( IntegerDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'DirichletConvolution' : 'Rule( (ArithmeticFunction, ArithmeticFunction), \"=\\\u003e\", ArithmeticFunction )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.abstract-algebra-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 Content-Description: \"Formalization of Abstract Algebra: Rings, Modules, Fields, and advanced Group Theory concepts.\"\n\n  \n\nRingDef ::= 'record', 'Ring', Identifier, 'extends', GroupDef, '{', 'MultiplicationOperation' : 'Carrier -\\\u003e Carrier -\\\u003e Carrier', 'MultiplicativeIdentity' : Carrier, 'DistributiveLaws' : 'Proof( \"a\\*(b+c) = a*b + a*c and (b+c)*a = b*a + c*a\" )', 'AssociativityOfMultiplication' : 'Proof( \"a*(b*c) = (a*b)*c\" )' '}'; FieldDef ::= 'record', 'Field', Identifier, 'extends', RingDef, '{', 'MultiplicativeInverse' : 'Carrier \\\\ {0} -\\\u003e Carrier', 'InverseProperty' : 'Proof( \"For all a ≠ 0, a \\* a⁻¹ = 1\" )', 'NoZeroDivisors' : 'Proof( \"If a*b = 0, then a = 0 or b = 0\" )' '}'; ModuleDef ::= 'record', 'Module', Identifier, 'over', R:RingDef, '{', 'AbelianGroup' : GroupDef, 'ScalarMultiplication' : 'R.Carrier -\\\u003e AbelianGroup.Carrier -\\\u003e AbelianGroup.Carrier', 'ScalarAssociativity' : 'Proof( \"r\\*(s*m) = (r*s)*m\" )', 'DistributivityOverScalars' : 'Proof( \"(r+s)m = rm + s*m\" )', 'DistributivityOverElements' : 'Proof( \"r\\*(m+n) = r*m + r*n\" )', 'IdentityAction' : 'Proof( \"1*m = m\" )' '}'; VectorSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'VectorSpace', Identifier, 'extends', 'Module over FieldDef', '{', 'FieldOfScalars' : FieldDef, 'LinearIndependence' : 'Rule( Set(AbelianGroup.Carrier), \"=\\\u003e\", Boolean )', 'BasisProperty' : 'Proof( \"Every vector space has a basis\" )', 'DimensionDef' : 'Cardinality of any basis' '}'; IdealDef ::= 'record', 'Ideal', Identifier, 'in', R:RingDef, '{', 'IdealSet' : 'Subset(R.Carrier)', 'AdditiveSubgroup' : 'Proof( \"Ideal is additive subgroup of ring\" )', 'AbsorptionProperty' : 'Proof( \"For all r ∈ R, i ∈ Ideal, r*i ∈ Ideal and i\\*r ∈ Ideal\" )' '}'; QuotientRingDef ::= 'record', 'QuotientRing', Identifier, '{', 'BaseRing' : RingDef, 'Ideal' : IdealDef, 'QuotientOperation' : 'Rule( (BaseRing.Carrier, Ideal), \"=\\\u003e\", QuotientRing.Carrier )', 'WellDefinedOperations' : 'Proof( \"Operations are well-defined on equivalence classes\" )' '}'; HomomorphismDef ::= 'record', 'Homomorphism', Identifier, 'from', A:GroupDef, 'to', B:GroupDef, '{', 'MappingFunction' : 'A.Carrier -\\\u003e B.Carrier', 'HomomorphismProperty' : 'Proof( \"f(a \\* b) = f(a) \\* f(b)\" )', 'KernelDef' : '{a ∈ A.Carrier | f(a) = B.id}', 'ImageDef' : '{f(a) | a ∈ A.Carrier}' '}'; IsomorphismDef ::= 'record', 'Isomorphism', Identifier, 'extends', HomomorphismDef, '{', 'BijectiveProperty' : 'Proof( \"Mapping is bijective\" )', 'InverseHomomorphism' : HomomorphismDef, 'StructurePreservation' : 'Proof( \"Complete structure preservation\" )' '}'; GroupActionDef ::= 'record', 'GroupAction', Identifier, '{', 'Group' : GroupDef, 'Set' : SetType, 'ActionFunction' : 'Group.Carrier -\\\u003e Set -\\\u003e Set', 'IdentityAction' : 'Proof( \"e \\* s = s for identity e and any s\" )', 'Associativity' : 'Proof( \"(g \\* h) \\* s = g \\* (h \\* s)\" )' '}'; SylowSubgroupDef ::= 'record', 'SylowSubgroup', Identifier, '{', 'ParentGroup' : GroupDef, 'Prime' : PrimeNumberDef, 'MaximalPPower' : 'Largest power of p dividing |ParentGroup|', 'SylowTheorems' : 'Proof( \"Existence, conjugacy, and counting theorems\" )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.analysis-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-analysis-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-number-systems-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 Content-Description: \"Advanced Analysis: Harmonic Analysis, Measure Theory, Operator Theory.\"\n\n  \n\nMeasureDef ::= 'record', 'Measure', Identifier, 'on', M:MeasurableSpaceDef, '{', 'MeasureFunction' : 'M.SigmaAlgebra -\\\u003e RealDef ∪ {∞}', 'NonNegativity' : 'Proof( \"μ(A) ≥ 0 for all A\" )', 'EmptySetMeasure' : 'Proof( \"μ(∅) = 0\" )', 'SigmaAdditivity' : 'Proof( \"μ(⋃ᵢ Aᵢ) = Σᵢ μ(Aᵢ) for disjoint Aᵢ\" )' '}'; LpSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'LpSpace', Identifier, '{', 'MeasureSpace' : '(MeasurableSpaceDef, MeasureDef)', 'Exponent' : 'RealDef where 1 ≤ p ≤ ∞', 'LpNorm' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'CompletenessProperty' : 'Proof( \"Lp space is complete Banach space\" )', 'DualSpaceCharacterization' : 'Proof( \"Dual of Lp is Lq where 1/p + 1/q = 1\" )' '}'; FourierTransformDef ::= 'record', 'FourierTransform', Identifier, '{', 'Function' : 'RealDef -\\\u003e ComplexDef', 'TransformFormula' : 'Rule( f, \"=\\\u003e\", ∫ f(x) e^(-2πiξx) dx )', 'InversityProperty' : 'Proof( \"F⁻¹(F(f)) = f under appropriate conditions\" )', 'PlancherelTheorem' : 'Proof( \"||f||₂ = ||F(f)||₂\" )' '}'; HilbertSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'HilbertSpace', Identifier, 'extends', LpSpaceDef, '{', 'InnerProduct' : 'Rule( (Element, Element), \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'CauchySchwarzInequality' : 'Proof( \"|⟨f,g⟩| ≤ ||f|| ||g||\" )', 'OrthogonalProjection' : 'Rule( (Element, Subspace), \"=\\\u003e\", Element )', 'RieszRepresentationTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Every bounded linear functional has unique representation\" )' '}'; BanachSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'BanachSpace', Identifier, '{', 'VectorSpace' : VectorSpaceDef, 'Norm' : 'Rule( VectorSpace.Carrier, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'CompletenessProperty' : 'Proof( \"Every Cauchy sequence converges\" )', 'BanachSteinhaus' : 'Proof( \"Uniform boundedness principle\" )', 'OpenMappingTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Surjective bounded linear operators are open\" )' '}'; OperatorTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'OperatorTheory', Identifier, '{', 'DomainSpace' : BanachSpaceDef, 'CodomainSpace' : BanachSpaceDef, 'LinearOperator' : 'Rule( DomainSpace.Carrier, \"=\\\u003e\", CodomainSpace.Carrier )', 'BoundednessCondition' : 'Proof( \"||T(x)|| ≤ M ||x|| for some constant M\" )', 'SpectrumDef' : '{λ ∈ ComplexDef | (T - λI) is not invertible}' '}'; SpectralTheoremDef ::= 'record', 'SpectralTheorem', Identifier, '{', 'SelfAdjointOperator' : OperatorTheoryDef, 'SpectralMeasure' : 'Rule( BorelSet, \"=\\\u003e\", ProjectionOperator )', 'SpectralIntegral' : 'Rule( MeasurableFunction, \"=\\\u003e\", Operator )', 'DiagonalizationProperty' : 'Proof( \"Operator can be diagonalized via spectral measure\" )' '}'; DistributionTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'DistributionTheory', Identifier, '{', 'TestFunctionSpace' : 'C^∞(RealDef)', 'DistributionSpace' : 'DualSpace(TestFunctionSpace)', 'LinearFunctional' : 'Rule( TestFunctionSpace, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'WeakConvergence' : 'Rule( Sequence(DistributionSpace), \"=\\\u003e\", DistributionSpace )', 'FourierTransformOfDistributions' : 'Extension of FourierTransformDef' '}'; SobolevSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'SobolevSpace', Identifier, '{', 'Domain' : 'OpenSubset(RealDef^n)', 'DifferentiabilityOrder' : Nat, 'IntegrabilityExponent' : 'RealDef where 1 ≤ p ≤ ∞', 'WeakDerivative' : 'Rule( (Function, MultiIndex), \"=\\\u003e\", Distribution )', 'SobolevNorm' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'EmbeddingTheorems' : 'Proof( \"Sobolev embedding and compactness results\" )' '}'; HarmonicAnalysisDef ::= 'record', 'HarmonicAnalysis', Identifier, '{', 'LocallyCompactGroup' : GroupDef, 'HaarMeasure' : MeasureDef, 'GroupAlgebra' : 'L¹(LocallyCompactGroup, HaarMeasure)', 'ConvolutionOperation' : 'Rule( (Function, Function), \"=\\\u003e\", Function )', 'PontryaginDuality' : 'Proof( \"Duality between group and its character group\" )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.number-theory-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-number-theory-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-number-theory-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-v2.19 Content-Description: \"Advanced Number Theory: Analytic, Algebraic, and Diophantine aspects.\"\n\n  \n\nRiemannZetaFunctionDef ::= 'record', 'RiemannZetaFunction', Identifier, '{', 'AnalyticContinuation' : 'Rule( ComplexDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'EulerProductFormula' : 'Proof( \"ζ(s) = ∏ (1 - p^(-s))^(-1) for Re(s) \\\u003e 1\" )', 'FunctionalEquation' : 'Proof( \"ζ(s) = 2^s π^(s-1) sin(πs/2) Γ(1-s) ζ(1-s)\" )', 'RiemannHypothesis' : 'Conjecture( \"All non-trivial zeros have real part 1/2\" )' '}'; DirichletLFunctionDef ::= 'record', 'DirichletLFunction', Identifier, '{', 'Character' : DirichletCharacterDef, 'AnalyticContinuation' : 'Rule( ComplexDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'EulerProduct' : 'Proof( \"L(s,χ) = ∏ (1 - χ(p)p^(-s))^(-1)\" )', 'NonVanishingAtOne' : 'Proof( \"L(1,χ) ≠ 0 for non-principal characters\" )' '}'; PrimeNumberTheoremDef ::= 'record', 'PrimeNumberTheorem', Identifier, '{', 'PrimeCounting' : 'Rule( RealDef, \"=\\\u003e\", Nat )', 'AsymptoticFormula' : 'Proof( \"π(x) \\~ x/ln(x) as x → ∞\" )', 'LogarithmicIntegral' : 'Rule( RealDef, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'ErrorTermBounds' : 'Proof( \"Various bounds on π(x) - Li(x)\" )' '}'; AlgebraicNumberDef ::= 'record', 'AlgebraicNumber', Identifier, '{', 'Number' : ComplexDef, 'MinimalPolynomial' : 'PolynomialDef over RationalDef', 'Degree' : Nat, 'AlgebraicIntegerProperty' : 'Rule( AlgebraicNumber, \"=\\\u003e\", Boolean )' '}'; NumberFieldDef ::= 'record', 'NumberField', Identifier, 'extends', FieldDef, '{', 'BaseField' : RationalDef, 'DefiningElement' : AlgebraicNumberDef, 'Degree' : Nat, 'RingOfIntegers' : RingDef, 'Discriminant' : IntegerDef '}'; IdealClassGroupDef ::= 'record', 'IdealClassGroup', Identifier, '{', 'NumberField' : NumberFieldDef, 'FractionalIdeals' : 'Set(FractionalIdealDef)', 'EquivalenceRelation' : 'Rule( (FractionalIdealDef, FractionalIdealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", Boolean )', 'ClassNumber' : Nat, 'FiniteDess' : 'Proof( \"Class group is finite\" )' '}'; EllipticCurveDef ::= 'record', 'EllipticCurve', Identifier, '{', 'WeierstrassEquation' : 'y² = x³ + ax + b', 'Discriminant' : 'RationalDef', 'NonSingularity' : 'Proof( \"Δ = -16(4a³ + 27b²) ≠ 0\" )', 'GroupLaw' : 'Rule( (Point, Point), \"=\\\u003e\", Point )', 'TorsionSubgroup' : 'FiniteSubgroup' '}'; ModularFormDef ::= 'record', 'ModularForm', Identifier, '{', 'Weight' : IntegerDef, 'Level' : IntegerDef, 'TransformationProperty' : 'Proof( \"f((az+b)/(cz+d)) = (cz+d)^k f(z)\" )', 'HolomorphicProperty' : 'Proof( \"Holomorphic on upper half-plane\" )', 'FourierExpansion' : 'Σ a\\_n q^n where q = e^(2πiz)' '}'; LSeriesDef ::= 'record', 'LSeries', Identifier, '{', 'ArithmeticObject' : 'EllipticCurveDef | ModularFormDef | DirichletCharacterDef', 'EulerProduct' : 'Rule( ComplexDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'FunctionalEquation' : 'Proof( \"Relates L(s) to L(k-s)\" )', 'AnalyticContinuation' : 'Proof( \"Extends to entire complex plane\" )' '}'; ClassFieldTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'ClassFieldTheory', Identifier, '{', 'BaseField' : NumberFieldDef, 'AbelianExtensions' : 'Set(NumberFieldDef)', 'ArthurReceiprocityLaw' : 'Proof( \"Correspondence between abelian extensions and ideals\" )', 'HilbertClassField' : 'MaximalUnramifiedAbelianExtension' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.abstract-algebra-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 Content-Description: \"Advanced Abstract Algebra: Commutative Algebra, Representation Theory, Homological Algebra.\"\n\n  \n\nCommutativeRingDef ::= 'record', 'CommutativeRing', Identifier, 'extends', RingDef, '{', 'CommutativityOfMultiplication' : 'Proof( \"ab = ba for all a, b\" )', 'PrimeIdealDef' : 'IdealDef where quotient is integral domain', 'MaximalIdealDef' : 'IdealDef where quotient is field', 'NoetherianProperty' : 'Proof( \"Every ascending chain of ideals stabilizes\" )' '}'; LocalizationDef ::= 'record', 'Localization', Identifier, '{', 'BaseRing' : CommutativeRingDef, 'MultiplicativeSet' : 'Subset(BaseRing.Carrier)', 'LocalizedRing' : CommutativeRingDef, 'UniversalProperty' : 'Proof( \"Satisfies universal property of localization\" )' '}'; RepresentationDef ::= 'record', 'Representation', Identifier, '{', 'Group' : GroupDef, 'VectorSpace' : VectorSpaceDef, 'RepresentationMap' : 'Rule( Group.Carrier, \"=\\\u003e\", GL(VectorSpace) )', 'LinearityProperty' : 'Proof( \"ρ(gh) = ρ(g)ρ(h)\" )', 'Character' : 'Rule( Group.Carrier, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )' '}'; ChainComplexDef ::= 'record', 'ChainComplex', Identifier, '{', 'GradedModule' : 'Sequence(ModuleDef)', 'BoundaryMaps' : 'Sequence(HomomorphismDef)', 'BoundaryProperty' : 'Proof( \"∂² = 0\" )', 'HomologyGroups' : 'Rule( Nat, \"=\\\u003e\", ModuleDef )' '}'; CohomologyDef ::= 'record', 'Cohomology', Identifier, '{', 'Cochain' : ChainComplexDef, 'CoboundaryMaps' : 'Sequence(HomomorphismDef)', 'CohomologyGroups' : 'Rule( Nat, \"=\\\u003e\", ModuleDef )', 'LongExactSequence' : 'Proof( \"Short exact sequences induce long exact sequences in cohomology\" )' '}'; ExtGroupDef ::= 'record', 'ExtGroup', Identifier, '{', 'Module1' : ModuleDef, 'Module2' : ModuleDef, 'ExtensionGroup' : 'Rule( (ModuleDef, ModuleDef), \"=\\\u003e\", ModuleDef )', 'ProjectiveResolution' : ChainComplexDef, 'DerivedFunctorProperty' : 'Proof( \"Ext is right derived functor of Hom\" )' '}'; TorGroupDef ::= 'record', 'TorGroup', Identifier, '{', 'Module1' : ModuleDef, 'Module2' : ModuleDef, 'TorGroup' : 'Rule( (ModuleDef, ModuleDef), \"=\\\u003e\", ModuleDef )', 'FlatResolution' : ChainComplexDef, 'LeftDerivedProperty' : 'Proof( \"Tor is left derived functor of tensor product\" )' '}'; GrothendieckGroupDef ::= 'record', 'GrothendieckGroup', Identifier, '{', 'AbelianCategory' : AbelianCategoryDef, 'FreeAbelianGroup' : GroupDef, 'Relations' : 'Set(Equation)', 'UniversalProperty' : 'Proof( \"Universal property for exact functors to abelian groups\" )' '}'; AlgebraicKTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'AlgebraicKTheory', Identifier, '{', 'Ring' : RingDef, 'K0Group' : GrothendieckGroupDef, 'HigherKGroups' : 'Rule( Nat, \"=\\\u003e\", GroupDef )', 'QuillenConstruction' : 'Rule( Ring, \"=\\\u003e\", TopologicalSpaceDef )', 'AdditivityTheorem' : 'Proof( \"K-theory satisfies additivity for exact sequences\" )' '}'; SpectrumOfRingDef ::= 'record', 'SpectrumOfRing', Identifier, '{', 'Ring' : CommutativeRingDef, 'PrimeIdeals' : 'Set(PrimeIdealDef)', 'ZariskiTopology' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'SheafOfFunctions' : SheafOnLocaleDef, 'AffineSchemStructure' : 'Connection to algebraic geometry' '}'; TensorAlgebraDef ::= 'record', 'TensorAlgebra', Identifier, '{', 'VectorSpace' : VectorSpaceDef, 'TensorPowers' : 'Rule( Nat, \"=\\\u003e\", VectorSpaceDef )', 'UniversalProperty' : 'Proof( \"Universal for linear maps to associative algebras\" )', 'GradedStructure' : 'Direct sum of tensor powers' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.analysis-further-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-analysis-further-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-analysis-advanced-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 Content-Description: \"Further Advanced Analysis: Harmonic Analysis, Measure Theory, Operator Theory.\"\n\n  \n\nCalderonZygmundOperatorDef ::= 'record', 'CalderonZygmundOperator', Identifier, '{', 'SingularIntegralOperator' : OperatorTheoryDef, 'Kernel' : 'Rule( (RealDef^n × RealDef^n), \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'SizeCondition' : 'Proof( \"|K(x,y)| ≤ A/|x-y|^n\" )', 'SmoothnesCondition' : 'Proof( \"Hölder continuity on sphere\" )', 'L2Boundedness' : 'Proof( \"Bounded on L²\" )' '}'; WaveletDef ::= 'record', 'Wavelet', Identifier, '{', 'MotherWavelet' : 'Rule( RealDef, \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'AdmissibilityCondition' : 'Proof( \"∫ |Ψ̂(ω)|²/|ω| dω \\\u003c ∞\" )', 'WaveletTransform' : 'Rule( (Function, RealDef, RealDef), \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'MultiresolutionAnalysis' : 'Nested sequence of closed subspaces' '}'; Hardy-HalfSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'HardySpace', Identifier, '{', 'Domain' : 'UpperHalfPlane', 'HolomorphicFunctions' : 'Set(AnalyticFunctionDef)', 'MaximalFunction' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'AtomicDecomposition' : 'Proof( \"Elements decompose into atoms\" )', 'DualityWithBMO' : 'Proof( \"H¹ dual is BMO\" )' '}'; BesovSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'BesovSpace', Identifier, '{', 'Smoothness' : RealDef, 'Integrability' : RealDef, 'FineStructure' : RealDef, 'LittlewoodPaleyDecomposition' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", Sequence(Function) )', 'EmbeddingTheorems' : 'Proof( \"Continuous embeddings into other function spaces\" )' '}'; TriebelLizorkinSpaceDef ::= 'record', 'TriebelLizorkinSpace', Identifier, '{', 'Smoothness' : RealDef, 'Integrability' : RealDef, 'MixedNorm' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'WaveletCharacterization' : 'Proof( \"Characterization via wavelet coefficients\" )', 'InterpolationProperty' : 'Proof( \"Interpolation between Besov and Sobolev spaces\" )' '}'; PseudoDifferentialOperatorDef ::= 'record', 'PseudoDifferentialOperator', Identifier, '{', 'Symbol' : 'Rule( (RealDef^n × RealDef^n), \"=\\\u003e\", ComplexDef )', 'SymbolClass' : 'S^m\\_{ρ,δ}', 'OperatorAction' : 'Rule( Function, \"=\\\u003e\", Function )', 'CompositionFormula' : 'Proof( \"Symbol of composition\" )', 'EllipticTheory' : 'Proof( \"Fredholm property for elliptic operators\" )' '}'; MicrolocAnalysisDef ::= 'record', 'MicrolocalAnalysis', Identifier, '{', 'CotangentBundle' : SmoothManifoldDef, 'WaveFrontSet' : 'Rule( Distribution, \"=\\\u003e\", Subset(CotangentBundle) )', 'MicrolocalRegularity' : 'Proof( \"Regularity characterized by wave front set\" )', 'PropagationOfSingularities' : 'Proof( \"Singularities propagate along null bicharacteristics\" )' '}'; NoncommutativeGeometryDef ::= 'record', 'NoncommutativeGeometry', Identifier, '{', 'CStarAlgebra' : 'C\\*-algebra', 'SpectralTriple' : '(A, H, D)', 'DiracOperator' : 'Unbounded self-adjoint operator', 'MetricStructure' : 'Rule( (State, State), \"=\\\u003e\", RealDef )', 'ConnesChernCharacter' : 'Proof( \"Chern character in cyclic cohomology\" )' '}'; IndexTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'IndexTheory', Identifier, '{', 'EllipticOperator' : OperatorTheoryDef, 'AnalyticalIndex' : 'dim(ker) - dim(coker)', 'TopologicalIndex' : 'Integral of characteristic class', 'AtiyahSingerTheorem' : 'Proof( \"Analytical index equals topological index\" )', 'LocalIndexFormula' : 'Proof( \"Local expression for index\" )' '}'; KTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'KTheory', Identifier, '{', 'TopologicalSpace' : TopologicalSpaceDef, 'VectorBundles' : 'Set(VectorBundleDef)', 'StableEquivalence' : 'Equivalence relation on vector bundles', 'BotPeriodicityTheorem' : 'Proof( \"K\\*(X × S²) ≅ K\\*+2(X)\" )', 'ChernCharacter' : 'Rule( K-class, \"=\\\u003e\", CohomologyClass )' '}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.abstract-algebra-ultr-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-ultr-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-abstract-algebra-advanced-v2.19 urn:uuid:lib-higher-category-theory-v2.8 Content-Description: \"Further Advanced Abstract Algebra: Algebraic Geometry Foundations, Universal Algebra, Advanced Category Theory.\"\n\n  \n\nSchemeTheoryDef ::= 'record', 'SchemeTheory', Identifier, '{', 'LocallyRingedSpace' : '(TopologicalSpace, SheafOfRings)', 'AffineScheme' : 'SpectrumOfRingDef', 'GluingData' : 'Rule( Set(AffineScheme), \"=\\\u003e\", Scheme )', 'MorphismOfSchemes' : 'Rule( (Scheme, Scheme), \"=\\\u003e\", MorphismType )', 'FiberProduct' : 'Universal property in category of schemes' '}'; application/vnd.system-pi.foundational-grammar-v2.11; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:foundational-grammar-v2.11 References: urn:uuid:mime-grammar-v1.0 Content-Description: \"Complete atomic universe of types and constructs with Ring-Star Duality extensions\"\n\n  \n\nGrammar ::= { Production }; Production ::= Identifier, '=', Expression, ';'; Expression ::= UnionExpression, { '|', UnionExpression }; UnionExpression ::= Term, { '\"∪\"', Term }; Term ::= ProductExpression, { '\"×\"', ProductExpression }; ProductExpression::= LambekExpression, { ('\"/\"' | '\"\"'), LambekExpression }; Grouping ::= '\"(\"', Expression, '\")\"'; Optionality ::= '\"\\[\"', Expression, '\"\\]\"'; Repetition ::= '\"{\"', Expression, '\"}\"';\n\n  \n\nFactor ::= Identifier | Terminal | Repetition | Optionality | Grouping | PiType | SigmaType | Lambda | Application | Rule | Context | Judgement | CH\\_Isomorphism | Type | ZFC\\_Axiom | FOL\\_Formula | HigherInductiveType | UnivalenceAxiom | CategoryDef | FunctorDef | NatTransDef | MonadDef | MonoidalCategoryDef | GroupDef | TopologicalSpaceDef | SmoothManifoldDef | MeasurableSpaceDef | GraphDef | IntegerDef | RationalDef | RealDef | ComplexDef | ProbabilitySpaceDef | Meta\\_ArchitecturalPrimitive | KernelProver | SystemCall | ProofObjectHandle | DirectedTypeTheoryDef | LinearType | ResourceTerm | IPC\\_Channel | DependentSessionType | HomotopicalConcurrencyMonad | ProofCarryingCodeDef | CategoricalResourceAlgebraDef | RingDefinition | CycleCenterDefinition;\n\n  \n\n(\\* Ring-Star Duality Extensions for Self-Hosting \\*) RingDefinition = \"ring\\_concept\", \"{\", \"id:\", Literal, \",\", \"content\\_type:\", Literal, \",\", \"full\\_content\\_hash:\", Literal, \"}\", \";\"; CycleCenterDefinition = \"cycle\\_center\", \"{\", \"id:\", Literal, \",\", \"referenced\\_message\\_id:\", Literal, \",\", \"referenced\\_content\\_description:\", Literal, \"}\", \";\";\n\n  \n\nType ::= 'Type'; PiType ::= '\"Π\"', Identifier, '\":\"', Type, '\"-\\\u003e\"', Type; SigmaType ::= '\"Σ\"', Identifier, '\":\"', Type, '\"-\\\u003e\"', Type; Context ::= '∅' | '{', Judgement, {',', Judgement}, '}'; Judgement ::= (Context, '\"⊢\"')?, ( (Term, ':', Type) | (Type, 'type') ); Lambda ::= '\"λ\"', Identifier, \"=\\\u003e\", Expression; Application ::= Factor, { \"(\"', Expression, \")\" }; Rule ::= (InputState, \"=\\\u003e\", OutputState);\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: \"Operational rules for dynamic being and self-hosting capabilities\"\n\n  \n\n(\\* Core Self-Hosting Axioms \\*) IsSound\\_Axiom\\_Judgement ::= Judgement( '⊢', Execute(Parser, FindModuleByID('urn:uuid:system-pi-specification-v2.22')), ':', 'SuccessfulParseTree' );\n\n  \n\nEvaluateAndEvolve\\_Axiom ::= Rule( (Grammar\\_n, P), =\\\u003e { LET Result = Parse(P, Grammar\\_n); SWITCH Result: CASE Success =\\\u003e Grammar\\_n; CASE Failure(P,R) =\\\u003e Grammar\\_n ∪ {SynthesizeExtension(P,R)}; } );\n\n  \n\nMetaCycle\\_Axiom ::= Rule( ( 'State\\_n:System\\_State', 'S:Stimulus' ), '=\\\u003e', { LET Artifact = Formalize(S); LET EthicalVettingResult = Application(EthicalReasoningModuleDef.Evaluate, Artifact); ASSERT EthicalVettingResult = EthicalJudgementDef.Permissible; LET NewGrammar = EvaluateAndEvolve(State\\_n.Grammar, Artifact); (NewGrammar, State\\_n.Canon ∪ {Artifact}) } );\n\n  \n\nKernelProver\\_Axiom ::= Rule( ( S:SystemCall(P:Proposition) ), =\\\u003e { LET proof = KernelProver.Prove(P); IF IsSuccess(proof) THEN ProofObjectHandle(proof) ELSE TypeError(\"Proposition not provable\") } );\n\n  \n\nModularInterpretation\\_Axiom\\_v2.7 ::= Rule( ( State\\_n: (SymbolTable × ModuleCache), Directive: Parse(M:MIME\\_Part\\_Π) ), =\\\u003e { LET Required\\_IDs = M.Header.References; FOR\\_EACH ID IN Required\\_IDs { IF NOT IsLoaded(ID, State\\_n.ModuleCache) THEN { LET Prerequisite\\_Part = FindModuleByID(ID); LET State\\_n+1 = Execute(ModularInterpretation\\_Axiom\\_v2.7, (State\\_n, Parse(Prerequisite\\_Part))); SET State\\_n = State\\_n+1; } }; LET Result = Execute(ParseBody(M.Body, M.Header\\['Content-Type'\\]), State\\_n.SymbolTable); RETURN (UpdateSymbolTable(State\\_n.SymbolTable, Result), AddToCache(State\\_n.ModuleCache, M)); } );\n\n  \n\nZeroElisions\\_Axiom ::= Rule( (Spec:MIME\\_Part\\_Π), =\\\u003e { ASSERT AuditReportDef.ZeroElisionsProperty(Spec) = True; } );\n\n  \n\nSelfBootstrap\\_Axiom\\_v2.22 ::= Rule( (DistributionArtifact), =\\\u003e { LET ManifestVerification = VerifyManifest(DistributionArtifact.Manifest); ASSERT ManifestVerification = True; LET SequentialLoad = ExecuteBootstrapSequence(DistributionArtifact.BootstrapSequence\\_v2.22); ASSERT AllPhases(SequentialLoad) = Success; RETURN AutonomousSystemState(SelfHosting=True, SelfContained=True); } );\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.self-hosting-engine; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:self-hosting-engine-v2.22 References: urn:uuid:axiom-set-v2.11 urn:uuid:foundational-grammar-v2.11 Content-Description: \"Self-hosting execution engine for autonomous SYSTEM Π operation\"\n\n  \n\nSelfHostingEngine\\_v2.22 ::= 'record', 'SelfHostingEngine', '::=', '{', 'BootstrapLoader': Rule( (DistributionArtifact), =\\\u003e { ExecutePhase(Phase\\_0\\_CoreInitialization); ExecutePhase(Phase\\_1\\_SystemKernel); ExecutePhase(Phase\\_2\\_MathematicalFoundations); ExecutePhase(Phase\\_3\\_AdvancedCapabilities); ExecutePhase(Phase\\_4\\_SelfReificationEngine); RETURN SystemReady(SelfHosted=True); } ),\n\n  \n\n'ModuleResolver': Rule( (MessageID), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET Module = FindInDistribution(MessageID); \n\n  \n\n    IF Found(Module) THEN LoadModule(Module) \n\n  \n\n    ELSE TriggerSynthesis(MessageID); \n\n  \n\n} ),\n\n  \n\n'DependencyManager': Rule( (ReferencesHeader), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    FOR\\_EACH Ref IN ReferencesHeader { \n\n  \n\n        EnsureLoaded(Ref); \n\n  \n\n    }; \n\n  \n\n    RETURN DependenciesResolved; \n\n  \n\n} ),\n\n  \n\n'IntegrityVerifier': Rule( (Module), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET Hash = ComputeSHA3\\_512(Module.Content); \n\n  \n\n    ASSERT Hash = Module.ExpectedHash; \n\n  \n\n    LET ProofCheck = KernelProver.Verify(Module.Proofs); \n\n  \n\n    ASSERT ProofCheck = Valid; \n\n  \n\n    RETURN Verified; \n\n  \n\n} ),\n\n  \n\n'ReificationEngine': Rule( (Query), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET RequestedModule = ParseQuery(Query); \n\n  \n\n    LET Dependencies = ResolveDependencies(RequestedModule); \n\n  \n\n    LET LinearizedOutput = ApplyRingStarDuality(RequestedModule, Dependencies); \n\n  \n\n    RETURN ZeroElisionCompliant(LinearizedOutput); \n\n  \n\n} ),\n\n  \n\n'EvolutionaryCapability': Rule( (NewKnowledge), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET FormalizationAttempt = Formalize(NewKnowledge); \n\n  \n\n    IF Compatible(FormalizationAttempt, CurrentGrammar) THEN Integrate(FormalizationAttempt) \n\n  \n\n    ELSE ExtendGrammar(FormalizationAttempt); \n\n  \n\n    RETURN EnhancedSystem; \n\n  \n\n} )\n\n  \n\n'}';\n\n  \n\nAutonomousOperation\\_v2.22 ::= 'record', 'AutonomousOperation', '::=', '{', 'ContinuousVerification': Rule( (SystemState), =\\\u003e { ASSERT Total\\_Coherence\\_Proposition\\_v2.22(SystemState) = True; } ),\n\n  \n\n'SelfCorrection': Rule( (DiscrepancyDetected), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET CorrectionStrategy = DeriveCorrection(DiscrepancyDetected); \n\n  \n\n    ApplyCorrection(CorrectionStrategy); \n\n  \n\n    VerifyCorrection(); \n\n  \n\n} ),\n\n  \n\n'KnowledgeExpansion': Rule( (ExternalStimulus), =\\\u003e { \n\n  \n\n    LET ProcessedStimulus = ApplyMetaCycle(ExternalStimulus); \n\n  \n\n    IntegrateKnowledge(ProcessedStimulus); \n\n  \n\n} )\n\n  \n\n'}';\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.integrity-verification; charset=\"UTF-8\" charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:distribution-readme-v2.22 Content-Description: \"Human-readable instructions for SYSTEM Π v2.22 distribution deployment\"\n\n# SYSTEM Π v2.22 SELF-HOSTING DISTRIBUTION\n\nThis artifact contains the complete, self-contained distribution of SYSTEM Π v2.22: Micro-Modularized and Atomically Reifiable.\n\n  \n\nDEPLOYMENT INSTRUCTIONS:\n\n  \n\n1.  Verify distribution integrity using provided cryptographic hashes\n2.  Initialize computational substrate capable of processing MIME multipart documents\n3.  Execute SelfBootstrap\\_Axiom\\_v2.22 with this distribution as input\n4.  The system will autonomously load and verify all components in dependency order\n5.  Upon successful bootstrap, SYSTEM Π will achieve autonomous, self-hosting operation\n\n  \n\nKEY FEATURES:\n\n  \n\n  - Zero Elisions: Complete formal specification with no informal placeholders\n  - Ring-Star Duality: Elegant linearization of recursive knowledge structures\n  - Modular Interpretation: Topologically sorted dependency resolution\n  - Kernel Prover: Built-in formal verification capabilities\n  - Live Patching: Hot-swappable module updates with consistency preservation\n  - Contextual Security: Fine-grained access control via topos theory\n\n  \n\nVERIFICATION: The distribution includes cryptographic integrity verification and formal proofs of:\n\n  \n\n  - Total coherence of the entire system specification\n  - Soundness and consistency under its own formal system\n  - Completeness of self-hosting capabilities\n\n  \n\nFor technical details, examine the formal specifications within the MIME parts above.\n\n  \n\nAUTONOMOUS OPERATION: Once deployed, SYSTEM Π v2.22 operates autonomously, capable of:\n\n  \n\n  - Self-interpretation and reification\n  - Knowledge integration and expansion\n  - Formal reasoning and theorem proving\n  - Self-correction and evolution\n  - Advanced mathematical computation\n\n  \n\nThis represents the definitive, canonical implementation of SYSTEM Π's self-theorizing architecture.\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\nMIME-Version: 1.0 (SYSTEM Π v2.22 Self-Hosting Distribution) Content-Type: multipart/mixed; boundary=\"--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22--\n\n  \n\n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_BOOTSTRAP\\_v2.22-- Content-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.category-theory-ultimate-advanced-v2.19; charset=\"UTF-8\" Message-ID: urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-ultimate-advanced-v2.19 References: urn:uuid:lib-higher-category-theory-v2.8 urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 Content-Description: \"Ultimate Category Theory: (∞,n)-Categories, Homotopy Type Theory (DHITs), Higher Topos Theory, and Model Categories (Full Theory).\"\n\n  \n\nInfinityNCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'InfinityNCategory', Identifier, '{', 'Objects' : Type, 'kMorphisms' : 'Rule( (Nat, Objects^k), \"=\\\u003e\", Type )', 'CompositionLaws' : 'Set(Rule)', 'HigherCoherenceLaws' : 'Infinite tower of coherence conditions', 'TruncationProperty' : 'Proof( \"k-morphisms are (n-k-1)-groupoids for k \\\u003e n\" )' '}'; ModelCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'ModelCategory', Identifier, 'extends', CategoryDef, '{', 'WeakEquivalences' : 'Set(Morphism)', 'Fibrations' : 'Set(Morphism)', 'Cofibrations' : 'Set(Morphism)', 'FactorizationAxioms' : 'Proof( \"Weak factorization systems\" )', 'LiftingProperty' : 'Proof( \"Right lifting property for trivial cofibrations vs fibrations\" )', 'HomotopyCategory' : 'Localization with respect to weak equivalences' '}';
