It serves as a complete self-hosting upgrade package.\" );  \n  \n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_UPGRADE\\_v2.22\\_ENHANCED\\_20250724--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.uuid-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:lib-number-systems-v2.3 urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Axiomatic definition of Universally Unique Identifiers (UUIDs) and their generation.\"  \n  \nUUID ::= 'record', 'UUIDType', Identifier, '{'  \n    'Bytes' : Sequence(ByteDef), (\\* A 16-byte (128-bit) sequence \\*)  \n    'Version' : Nat, (\\* Version of UUID (e.g., 1, 4, 5) \\*)  \n    'Variant' : Nat (\\* Variant (e.g., RFC 4122) \\*)  \n'}';  \n  \nUUID.generate ::= 'Rule', 'GenerateUUID', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'UnitType',  \n    'Output' : 'NewUUID:UUID',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ \\_. {  \n        RETURN UUIDType(  \n            Bytes = Rule.execute(Kernel.GenerateRandomBytes, 16).Output.Bytes,  \n            Version = 4,  \n            Variant = 2  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'UniquenessAxiom' : 'Proof( \"Π (u1:UUID, u2:UUID), (Kernel.GenerateRandomBytes(16) concurrently executed) → (Prob(u1 = u2) \\\u003c 2^-122)\" )';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'Proof( \"Generated UUID conforms to RFC 4122 specification for its version and variant.\" )'  \n'}';  \n  \nUUID.generate\\_from\\_hash ::= 'Rule', 'GenerateUUIDFromHash', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'Hash:StringDef', (\\* A SHA-3/512 hash string \\*)  \n    'Output' : 'NewUUID:UUID',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ hash\\_str. {  \n        LET hash\\_bytes = Rule.execute(StringDef.to\\_datastream, hash\\_str).Output.DataStreamDef;  \n        LET truncated\\_bytes = Rule.execute(DataStream.get\\_segment, (hash\\_bytes, 0, 16)).Output.Segment; (\\* Take first 16 bytes for UUID \\*)  \n        RETURN UUIDType(  \n            Bytes = truncated\\_bytes,  \n            Version = 5, (\\* Represents name-based UUID using SHA-1, but can be adapted for SHA3 \\*)  \n            Variant = 2  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'DeterministicProperty' : 'Proof( \"Π (h:StringDef), GenerateUUIDFromHash(h) is deterministic.\" )';  \n    'CollisionResistanceProperty' : 'Proof( \"Collision resistance inherited from underlying hash function.\" )';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'Proof( \"Generated UUID conforms to RFC 4122 specification for name-based UUIDs.\" )'  \n'}';  \n  \nUUID.generate\\_deterministic ::= 'Rule', 'GenerateDeterministicUUID', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'NamespaceString:StringDef', (\\* A string to deterministically generate UUID from \\*)  \n    'Output' : 'NewUUID:UUID',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ ns\\_str. {  \n        LET hash = Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, StringDef.to\\_datastream(ns\\_str)).Output.StringDef;  \n        RETURN Rule.execute(UUID.generate\\_from\\_hash, hash).Output.NewUUID;  \n    }';  \n    'DeterministicProperty' : 'Proof( \"Π (ns1:StringDef, ns2:StringDef), (ns1 = ns2) → (GenerateDeterministicUUID(ns1) = GenerateDeterministicUUID(ns2))\" )';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'Proof( \"Generated UUID is deterministic for given namespace string.\" )'  \n'}';  \n  \nKernel.GenerateRandomBytes ::= 'Rule', 'GenerateRandomBytesPrimitive', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'Count:Nat',  \n    'Output' : 'Bytes:Sequence(ByteDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ count. { (\\* System Call to underlying substrate\\\\'s CSPRNG \\*) }';  \n    'RandomnessAxiom' : 'Proof( \"Generated bytes are cryptographically secure pseudo-random.\" )'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_UPGRADE\\_v2.22\\_ENHANCED\\_20250724--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.system-state-management-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-plan9-model-v1.0-dagger urn:uuid:lib-tiered-math-foundations-v2.2 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-topology-v2.16  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definitions for SystemStateDef atomic update rules and general data structure operations.\"  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateFSTree', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewTree:DirectoryDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_tree). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = new\\_tree,  \n            ProcessTable = old\\_state.ProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = old\\_state.NetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = old\\_state.DeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = old\\_state.EpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = old\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateFSTreeCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_process\\_table ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateProcessTable', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewTable:Map(PIDDef, ProcessDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_table). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = old\\_state.FilesystemTree,  \n            ProcessTable = new\\_table,  \n            NetworkState = old\\_state.NetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = old\\_state.DeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = old\\_state.EpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = old\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateProcessTableCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_network\\_state ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateNetworkState', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewState:Set(NetworkConnectionDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_net\\_state). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = old\\_state.FilesystemTree,  \n            ProcessTable = old\\_state.ProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = new\\_net\\_state,  \n            DeviceStates = old\\_state.DeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = old\\_state.EpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = old\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateNetworkStateCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_device\\_states ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateDeviceStates', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewStates:Map(DeviceIDDef, DeviceStateDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_dev\\_states). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = old\\_state.FilesystemTree,  \n            ProcessTable = old\\_state.ProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = old\\_state.NetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = new\\_dev\\_states,  \n            EpistemicStates = old\\_state.EpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = old\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateDeviceStatesCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateEpistemicStates', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewStates:Map(ObserverIDDef, EpistemicStateDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_epi\\_states). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = old\\_state.FilesystemTree,  \n            ProcessTable = old\\_state.ProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = old\\_state.NetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = old\\_state.DeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = new\\_epi\\_states,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = old\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateEpistemicStatesCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_global\\_log ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateGlobalLog', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewLog:TransactionLogDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_log). {  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = old\\_state.FilesystemTree,  \n            ProcessTable = old\\_state.ProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = old\\_state.NetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = old\\_state.DeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = old\\_state.EpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = new\\_log,  \n            MountTable = old\\_state.MountTable,  \n            AllSystemResources = old\\_state.AllSystemResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateGlobalLogCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.update\\_shell\\_state ::= 'Rule', 'UpdateShellState', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldState:SystemStateDef, NewShell:Plan9ShellDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'UpdatedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (old\\_state, new\\_shell). {  \n        LET updated\\_process\\_table = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (old\\_state.ProcessTable, new\\_shell.ShellProcess.ID, new\\_shell.ShellProcess)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n        RETURN Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_process\\_table, (old\\_state, updated\\_process\\_table)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:UpdateShellStateCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\-- Delta Application Rules  \nSystemStateDef.apply\\_delta\\_backward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyDeltaBackward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentState:SystemStateDef, Delta:ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'RestoredState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_state, delta). {  \n        LET RestoredAllResources = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_resource\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.AllSystemResources, delta.FileSystemDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n        LET RestoredFSTree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.apply\\_filesystem\\_tree\\_structure\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.FilesystemTree, delta.FileSystemDelta, RestoredAllResources)).Output.RestoredTree;  \n        LET RestoredProcessTable = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_process\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.ProcessTable, delta.ProcessTableDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n        LET RestoredNetworkState = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_network\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.NetworkState, delta.NetworkStateDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n        LET RestoredDeviceStates = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_device\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.DeviceStates, delta.DeviceStatesDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n        LET RestoredEpistemicStates = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_epistemic\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.EpistemicStates, delta.EpistemicStatesDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n        LET RestoredMountTable = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_mount\\_table\\_map\\_delta\\_backward, (current\\_state.MountTable.Entries, delta.MountTableDelta)).Output.RestoredMap;  \n  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = RestoredFSTree,  \n            ProcessTable = RestoredProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = RestoredNetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = RestoredDeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = RestoredEpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = current\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = MountTableDef(Entries = RestoredMountTable),  \n            AllSystemResources = RestoredAllResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'DeltaReversalProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyDeltaBackwardProof\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nSystemStateDef.apply\\_delta\\_forward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyDeltaForward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentState:SystemStateDef, Delta:ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'AppliedState:SystemStateDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_state, delta). {  \n        LET AppliedAllResources = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_resource\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.AllSystemResources, delta.FileSystemDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n        LET AppliedFSTree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.apply\\_filesystem\\_tree\\_structure\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.FilesystemTree, delta.FileSystemDelta, AppliedAllResources)).Output.AppliedTree;  \n        LET AppliedProcessTable = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_process\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.ProcessTable, delta.ProcessTableDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n        LET AppliedNetworkState = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_network\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.NetworkState, delta.NetworkStateDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n        LET AppliedDeviceStates = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_device\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.DeviceStates, delta.DeviceStatesDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n        LET AppliedEpistemicStates = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_epistemic\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.EpistemicStates, delta.EpistemicStatesDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n        LET AppliedMountTable = Rule.execute(Map.apply\\_mount\\_table\\_map\\_delta\\_forward, (current\\_state.MountTable.Entries, delta.MountTableDelta)).Output.AppliedMap;  \n  \n        RETURN SystemStateDef(  \n            FilesystemTree = AppliedFSTree,  \n            ProcessTable = AppliedProcessTable,  \n            NetworkState = AppliedNetworkState,  \n            DeviceStates = AppliedDeviceStates,  \n            EpistemicStates = AppliedEpistemicStates,  \n            GlobalTransactionLog = current\\_state.GlobalTransactionLog,  \n            MountTable = MountTableDef(Entries = AppliedMountTable),  \n            AllSystemResources = AppliedAllResources  \n        );  \n    }';  \n    'DeltaApplicationProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyDeltaForwardProof\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n  \n(\\* Reified Delta Application for Maps (e.g., FileSystemDelta, ProcessTableDelta, etc.) \\*)  \nMap.apply\\_resource\\_map\\_delta\\_backward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyResourceMapDeltaBackward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentMap:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource), Delta:Map(Plan9ResourceID, ResourceSnapshotDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'RestoredMap:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_map, delta). {  \n        LET working\\_map = current\\_map;  \n        FOR\\_EACH res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET res\\_id = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldResourceSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                (\\* Resource existed, revert to old state \\*)  \n                LET old\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET restored\\_resource = IF old\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(FileDef) THEN  \n                                          FileDef(ID=old\\_snapshot.ResourceID, Content=old\\_snapshot.Content.Value, Metadata=old\\_snapshot.Metadata)  \n                                        ELSE IF old\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(DirectoryDef) THEN  \n                                          DirectoryDef(ID=old\\_snapshot.ResourceID, Entries=old\\_snapshot.Entries.Value, Parent=old\\_snapshot.Parent.Value)  \n                                        ELSE IF old\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(SemanticNodeDef) THEN  \n                                          SemanticNodeDef(ID=old\\_snapshot.ResourceID, FormalContentID=old\\_snapshot.FormalContentID.Value, NodeType=old\\_snapshot.NodeType.Value, RepresentationType=old\\_snapshot.RepresentationType.Value, Description=old\\_snapshot.Description.Value, ChildNodes=old\\_snapshot.ChildNodes.Value)  \n                                        ELSE  \n                                          Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (current\\_map, res\\_id)).Output.Option.Value  \n                                        END IF;  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (working\\_map, res\\_id, restored\\_resource)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            ELSE (\\* IsInstanceOf(snapshot, NewResourceSnapshotDef) \\*)  \n                (\\* Resource was added, so remove it \\*)  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (working\\_map, res\\_id)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n        RETURN working\\_map;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyResourceMapDeltaBackwardCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nMap.apply\\_resource\\_map\\_delta\\_forward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyResourceMapDeltaForward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentMap:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource), Delta:Map(Plan9ResourceID, ResourceSnapshotDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'AppliedMap:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_map, delta). {  \n        LET working\\_map = current\\_map;  \n        FOR\\_EACH res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET res\\_id = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, NewResourceSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                (\\* Resource was modified or created, apply new state \\*)  \n                LET new\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET applied\\_resource = IF new\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(FileDef) THEN  \n                                         FileDef(ID=new\\_snapshot.ResourceID, Content=new\\_snapshot.Content.Value, Metadata=new\\_snapshot.Metadata)  \n                                       ELSE IF new\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(DirectoryDef) THEN  \n                                         DirectoryDef(ID=new\\_snapshot.ResourceID, Entries=new\\_snapshot.Entries.Value, Parent=new\\_snapshot.Parent.Value)  \n                                       ELSE IF new\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(SemanticNodeDef) THEN  \n                                         SemanticNodeDef(ID=new\\_snapshot.ResourceID, FormalContentID=new\\_snapshot.FormalContentID.Value, NodeType=new\\_snapshot.NodeType.Value, RepresentationType=new\\_snapshot.RepresentationType.Value, Description=new\\_snapshot.Description.Value, ChildNodes=new\\_snapshot.ChildNodes.Value)  \n                                       ELSE  \n                                         Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (current\\_map, res\\_id)).Output.Option.Value  \n                                       END IF;  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (working\\_map, res\\_id, applied\\_resource)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            ELSE (\\* IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldResourceSnapshotDef) \\*)  \n                (\\* Resource was removed, so remove it from current map \\*)  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (working\\_map, res\\_id)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n        RETURN working\\_map;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyResourceMapDeltaForwardCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n(\\* Similar reified delta application rules for Process, Network, Device, Epistemic, and MountTable maps \\*)  \nMap.apply\\_process\\_map\\_delta\\_backward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyProcessMapDeltaBackward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentMap:Map(PIDDef, ProcessDef), Delta:Map(PIDDef, ProcessSnapshotDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'RestoredMap:Map(PIDDef, ProcessDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_map, delta). {  \n        LET working\\_map = current\\_map;  \n        FOR\\_EACH id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET id = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldProcessSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                LET old\\_snap = snapshot;  \n                LET restored\\_val = ProcessDef(ID=old\\_snap.ProcessID, State=old\\_snap.State, MemoryMap=old\\_snap.MemoryMap, FileHandles=old\\_snap.FileHandles);  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (working\\_map, id, restored\\_val)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            ELSE  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (working\\_map, id)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n        FOR\\_EACH id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET id = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldMountTableSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                LET old\\_snap = snapshot;  \n                let restored\\_val = old\\_snap.Entries.lookup(id).Value;  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (working\\_map, id, restored\\_val)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            ELSE  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (working\\_map, id)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n        RETURN working\\_map;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyMountTableMapDeltaBackwardCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nMap.apply\\_mount\\_table\\_map\\_delta\\_forward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyMountTableMapDeltaForward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentMap:Map(UUID, MountTableEntryDef), Delta:Map(UUID, MountTableSnapshotDef))',  \n    'Output' : 'AppliedMap:Map(UUID, MountTableEntryDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_map, delta). {  \n        LET working\\_map = current\\_map;  \n        FOR\\_EACH id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET id = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (working\\_map, id, applied\\_val)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n            ELSE  \n                working\\_map = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (working\\_map, id)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n  \n(\\* Specific rules for applying deltas to the filesystem tree structure, operating on IDs, with knowledge of global resources \\*)  \nDirectoryDef.apply\\_filesystem\\_tree\\_structure\\_delta\\_backward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyFSTreeStructureDeltaBackward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentTree:DirectoryDef, Delta:Map(Plan9ResourceID, ResourceSnapshotDef), GlobalResources:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource))',  \n    'Output' : 'RestoredTree:DirectoryDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_tree, delta, global\\_resources). {  \n        LET working\\_tree = current\\_tree;  \n        FOR\\_EACH res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET res\\_id = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldResourceSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                LET old\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET current\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (global\\_resources, res\\_id)).Output.Option;  \n                LET path\\_to\\_resource = old\\_snapshot.LocationPath;  \n                LET parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.ParentPath;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET parent\\_path = parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n                LET leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.LeafName;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET leaf\\_name = leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n                IF Not(IsSome(current\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean) THEN (\\* Resource was removed by ForwardOp, needs to be re-added to tree \\*)  \n                    working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (working\\_tree, parent\\_path, leaf\\_name, res\\_id, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n                ELSE (\\* Resource existed, but its internal structure might have changed (e.g., directory entries) \\*)  \n                    IF old\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(DirectoryDef) THEN  \n                        LET restored\\_dir\\_entries = old\\_snapshot.Entries.Value;  \n                        LET current\\_dir = current\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n                        ASSERT IsInstanceOf(current\\_dir, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n                        IF Not(Rule.execute(Map.equals, (current\\_dir.Entries, restored\\_dir\\_entries)).Output.Boolean) THEN (\\* Only update if different \\*)  \n                           working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.replace\\_resource, (working\\_tree, res\\_id, DirectoryDef(ID=res\\_id, Entries=restored\\_dir\\_entries, Parent=current\\_dir.Parent).ID, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n                        END IF;  \n                    END IF;  \n                END IF;  \n            ELSE (\\* IsInstanceOf(snapshot, NewResourceSnapshotDef) \\*)  \n                (\\* Resource was added by ForwardOp, needs to be removed from tree structure \\*)  \n                LET new\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET path\\_to\\_resource = new\\_snapshot.LocationPath;  \n                LET parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.ParentPath;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET parent\\_path = parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n                LET leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.LeafName;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET leaf\\_name = leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n                working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.remove\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (working\\_tree, parent\\_path, leaf\\_name, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n        RETURN working\\_tree;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyFSTreeStructureDeltaBackwardCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nDirectoryDef.apply\\_filesystem\\_tree\\_structure\\_delta\\_forward ::= 'Rule', 'ApplyFSTreeStructureDeltaForward', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentTree:DirectoryDef, Delta:Map(Plan9ResourceID, ResourceSnapshotDef), GlobalResources:Map(Plan9ResourceID, Plan9Resource))',  \n    'Output' : 'AppliedTree:DirectoryDef',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (current\\_tree, delta, global\\_resources). {  \n        LET working\\_tree = current\\_tree;  \n        FOR\\_EACH res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, delta).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET res\\_id = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.first;  \n            LET snapshot = res\\_id\\_snapshot\\_pair.second;  \n  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(snapshot, NewResourceSnapshotDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                LET new\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET current\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (global\\_resources, res\\_id)).Output.Option;  \n                LET path\\_to\\_resource = new\\_snapshot.LocationPath;  \n                LET parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.ParentPath;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET parent\\_path = parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n                LET leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.LeafName;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET leaf\\_name = leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n                IF Not(IsSome(current\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean) THEN (\\* Resource was added, needs to be added to tree \\*)  \n                    working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (working\\_tree, parent\\_path, leaf\\_name, res\\_id, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n                ELSE (\\* Resource existed, but its internal structure might have changed (e.g., directory entries) \\*)  \n                    IF new\\_snapshot.ResourceType = TypeOf(DirectoryDef) THEN  \n                        LET new\\_dir\\_entries = new\\_snapshot.Entries.Value;  \n                        LET current\\_dir = current\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n                        ASSERT IsInstanceOf(current\\_dir, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n                        IF Not(Rule.execute(Map.equals, (current\\_dir.Entries, new\\_dir\\_entries)).Output.Boolean) THEN (\\* Only update if different \\*)  \n                           working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.replace\\_resource, (working\\_tree, res\\_id, DirectoryDef(ID=res\\_id, Entries=new\\_dir\\_entries, Parent=current\\_dir.Parent).ID, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n                        END IF;  \n                    END IF;  \n                END IF;  \n            ELSE (\\* IsInstanceOf(snapshot, OldResourceSnapshotDef) \\*)  \n                (\\* Resource was removed, needs to be removed from tree structure \\*)  \n                LET old\\_snapshot = snapshot;  \n                LET path\\_to\\_resource = old\\_snapshot.LocationPath;  \n                LET parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.ParentPath;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET parent\\_path = parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n                LET leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, path\\_to\\_resource).Output.LeafName;  \n                ASSERT IsSome(leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET leaf\\_name = leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n                working\\_tree = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.remove\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (working\\_tree, parent\\_path, leaf\\_name, global\\_resources)).Output.UpdatedTree;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n        RETURN working\\_tree;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:ApplyFSTreeStructureDeltaForwardCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n  \n(\\* Auxiliary Data Structure Operations (concrete definitions) - re-iterating for completeness and adding new ones \\*)  \nMap.empty ::= 'Rule((), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", Map)'; Map.empty := 'λ \\_ . Rule.execute(Map.insert, (Rule.execute(Map.remove, (m, k)).Output, k, v)).Output';  \nMap.to\\_list ::= 'Rule((M:Map), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", Sequence(Pair(Key, Value)))'; Map.to\\_list := 'λ m . AxiomaticIsInstanceOf(t, ty)';  \n  \n\\-- Path Operations (Re-iterating for completeness, adding append\\_name)  \nPath.resolve ::= 'Rule', 'PathResolve', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(Base:PathDef, Relative:StringDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'Result(PathDef, PathErrorDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (base, relative\\_str). {  \n        LET components = Rule.execute(StringDef.split, (relative\\_str, \"/\")).Output.Sequence;  \n        LET current\\_path = base;  \n        FOR\\_EACH comp IN Rule.execute(Sequence.to\\_list, components).Output.Sequence {  \n            SWITCH comp:  \n                CASE \".\":  \n                    current\\_path = current\\_path;  \n                CASE \"..\":  \n                    LET parent\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, current\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n                    IF IsNone(parent\\_opt).Output.Boolean THEN RETURN Err(PathErrorDef(\"Cannot resolve '..' beyond root\")) END IF;  \n                    current\\_path = parent\\_opt.Value;  \n                DEFAULT:  \n                    current\\_path = Rule.execute(Sequence.append, (current\\_path, Rule.execute(Sequence.singleton, comp).Output.Sequence)).Output.Sequence;  \n            END SWITCH;  \n        };  \n        RETURN Ok(current\\_path);  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:PathResolveCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nPath.parent ::= 'Rule', 'PathParent', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'P:PathDef',  \n    'Output' : 'ParentPath:Option(PathDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ p. {  \n        IF Rule.execute(Sequence.is\\_empty, p).Output.Boolean THEN RETURN None() END IF;  \n        RETURN Some(Rule.execute(Sequence.remove\\_last, p).Output.Sequence);  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:PathParentCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nPath.leaf ::= 'Rule', 'PathLeaf', '{'  \n    'Input' : 'P:PathDef',  \n    'Output' : 'LeafName:Option(NameDef)',  \n    'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ p. {  \n        IF Rule.execute(Sequence.is\\_empty, p).Output.Boolean THEN RETURN None() END IF; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:lib-category-extension-dagger-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:lib-category-theory-v2.1 urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"General framework for DaggerCategoryDef and ReversibleXOperationDef, enabling recursive dagger promotion.\"  \nBaseReversibleOperationDef ::= 'record', 'BaseReversibleOperation', Identifier, '{'  \n'ID' : UUID, (\\* Unique ID for this operation definition instance \\*)  \n'ForwardOp' : Rule,  \n'BackwardOp' : Rule,  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'Proof( \"ForwardOp composed with BackwardOp is identity in relevant context\" )',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'Proof( \"BackwardOp composed with ForwardOp is identity in relevant context\" )',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'Proof( \"The BackwardOp of BackwardOp is ForwardOp\" )'  \n'}';  \nDaggerCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'DaggerCategory', Identifier, 'extends', CategoryDef, '{'  \n'BaseCategory' : CategoryDef,  \n'DaggerFunctor' : 'Functor(BaseCategory^op, BaseCategory)',  \n'DaggerInvolution' : 'Proof( \"Π(f:BaseCategory.Hom a b), (f†)† = f\" )',  \n'DaggerAntiHomomorphismComposition' : 'Proof( \"Π({a,b,c:BaseCategory.Obj}, f:BaseCategory.Hom b c, g:BaseCategory.Hom a b), (BaseCategory.compose f g)† = (g†) ∘ (f†)\" )'  \n'}';  \nDaggerMonoidalCategoryDef ::= 'record', 'DaggerMonoidalCategory', Identifier, 'extends', MonoidalCategoryDef, '{'  \n'BaseMonoidalCategory' : MonoidalCategoryDef,  \n'DaggerFunctor' : 'Functor(BaseMonoidalCategory^op, BaseMonoidalCategory)',  \n'DaggerInvolution' : 'Proof( \"Π(f:BaseMonoidalCategory.Hom a b), (f†)† = f\" )',  \n'DaggerAntiHomomorphismComposition' : 'Proof( \"Π({a,b,c:BaseMonoidalCategory.Obj}, f:BaseMonoidalCategory.Hom b c, g:BaseMonoidalCategory.Hom a b), (BaseMonoidalCategory.compose f g)† = (g†) ∘ (f†)\" )',  \n'DaggerTensorProductCompatibility' : 'Proof( \"Π({a,b,c,d:BaseMonoidalCategory.Obj}, f:BaseMonoidalCategory.Hom a c, g:BaseMonoidalCategory.Hom b d), (f ⊗ g)† = (f†) ⊗ (g†)\" )'  \n'}';  \nFunctorDaggerPreservationDef ::= 'record', 'FunctorDaggerPreservation', Identifier, 'extends', FunctorDef, '{'  \n'Dom' : DaggerCategoryDef,  \n'Cod' : DaggerCategoryDef,  \n'preserves\\_dagger': 'Proof( \"Π(f:Dom.Hom a b), F\\_Map(f†) = (F\\_Map(f))†\" )'  \n'}';  \n\\--SYSTEM\\_PI\\_UPGRADE\\_v2.22\\_ENHANCED\\_20250724--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.library.epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified  \nReferences: urn:uuid:lib-foundational-logic-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-topology-v2.16 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formalized and proven InferenceEngineDef for reversible epistemic inference.\"  \nInferenceRuleDef ::= 'record', 'InferenceRule', Identifier, '{'  \n'ID' : UUID,  \n'Premises' : Set(Proposition),  \n'Conclusion' : Proposition,  \n'SoundnessProof' : 'Proof( \"Conclusion logically follows from Premises (derived from underlying FOL axioms).\" )'  \n'}';  \nDerivationGraphDef ::= 'record', 'DerivationGraph', Identifier, 'extends', GraphDef, '{'  \n'Vertices' : Set(Proposition),  \n'Edges' : Set(Pair(Proposition, Proposition))  \n'}';  \nInferenceEngineDef ::= 'record', 'InferenceEngine', Identifier, '{'  \n'RuleSet' : Set(InferenceRuleDef),  \n'Deduce' : 'Rule( (KnownFacts:Set(Proposition), RuleSet:Self.RuleSet), \"=\\\u003e\", (NewFacts:Set(Proposition), DerivationGraph:DerivationGraphDef) )',  \n'Deduce' := 'λ (known\\_facts, rule\\_set). {  \nLET deducible\\_facts = Rule.execute(Set.clone, known\\_facts).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nLET derivation\\_graph = Rule.execute(GraphDef.empty, UnitType).Output.GraphDef;  \nLET queue = Rule.execute(Queue.from\\_list, Rule.execute(Set.to\\_list, known\\_facts).Output.Sequence).Output.Queue;  \nWHILE Not(Rule.execute(Queue.is\\_empty, queue).Output.Boolean) {  \nLET popped\\_opt = Rule.execute(Queue.pop, queue).Output.Option;  \nASSERT IsSome(popped\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \nLET current\\_fact, updated\\_queue = popped\\_opt.Value.first, popped\\_opt.Value.second;  \nqueue = updated\\_queue;  \nIF Not(Rule.execute(Set.member, (current\\_fact, deducible\\_facts)).Output.Boolean) THEN  \ndeducible\\_facts = Rule.execute(Set.add, (deducible\\_facts, current\\_fact)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nEND IF;  \nFOR\\_EACH rule IN Rule.execute(Set.to\\_list, rule\\_set).Output.Sequence {  \nIF Rule.execute(Set.is\\_subset, (rule.Premises, deducible\\_facts)).Output.Boolean THEN  \nIF Not(Rule.execute(Set.member, (rule.Conclusion, deducible\\_facts)).Output.Boolean) THEN  \ndeducible\\_facts = Rule.execute(Set.add, (deducible\\_facts, rule.Conclusion)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nqueue = Rule.execute(Queue.push, (queue, rule.Conclusion)).Output.UpdatedQueue;  \nderivation\\_graph = Rule.execute(GraphDef.add\\_edge, (derivation\\_graph, current\\_fact, rule.Conclusion)).Output.UpdatedGraph;  \nEND IF;  \nEND IF;  \n};  \n};  \nRETURN (Rule.execute(Set.difference, (deducible\\_facts, known\\_facts)).Output.UpdatedSet, derivation\\_graph);  \n}',  \n'Retract' : 'Rule( (KnownFacts:Set(Proposition), FactToRetract:Proposition, RuleSet:Self.RuleSet, DerivationGraph:DerivationGraphDef), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", UpdatedFacts:Set(Proposition) )',  \n'Retract' := 'λ (known\\_facts, fact\\_to\\_retract, rule\\_set, derivation\\_graph). {  \nLET causally\\_dependent\\_facts = Rule.execute(GetCausallyDependentFactsFromGraph, (derivation\\_graph, fact\\_to\\_retract)).Output.DependentFacts;  \nLET facts\\_to\\_remove = Rule.execute(Set.union, (Rule.execute(Set.singleton, fact\\_to\\_retract).Output.Set, causally\\_dependent\\_facts)).Output.Set;  \nRETURN Rule.execute(Set.difference, (known\\_facts, facts\\_to\\_remove)).Output.Set;  \n}',  \n'InferenceSoundness' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:InferenceSoundness\\_20250724)',  \n'RetractionConsistency' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:RetractionConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nGetCausallyDependentFactsFromGraph ::= 'Rule', 'GetDependentFacts', '{'  \n'Input' : '(Graph:DerivationGraphDef, StartFact:Proposition)',  \n'Output' : 'DependentFacts:Set(Proposition)',  \n'ExecutionLogic' : 'λ (graph, start\\_fact). {  \nLET dependent\\_facts = Rule.execute(Set.empty, UnitType).Output.Set;  \nLET queue = Rule.execute(Queue.empty, UnitType).Output.Queue;  \nRule.execute(Queue.push, (queue, start\\_fact)).Output.UpdatedQueue;  \nWHILE Not(Rule.execute(Queue.is\\_empty, queue).Output.Boolean) {  \nLET popped\\_opt = Rule.execute(Queue.pop, queue).Output.Option;  \nASSERT IsSome(popped\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \nLET current\\_fact, updated\\_queue = popped\\_opt.Value.first, popped\\_opt.Value.second;  \nqueue = updated\\_queue;  \nIF Not(Rule.execute(Set.member, (current\\_fact, dependent\\_facts)).Output.Boolean) THEN  \ndependent\\_facts = Rule.execute(Set.add, (dependent\\_facts, current\\_fact)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nFOR\\_EACH successor\\_fact IN Rule.execute(Set.to\\_list, Rule.execute(DerivationGraphDef.get\\_successors, (graph, current\\_fact)).Output.Successors).Output.Sequence {  \nqueue = Rule.execute(Queue.push, (queue, successor\\_fact)).Output.UpdatedQueue;  \n};  \nEND IF;  \n};  \nRETURN dependent\\_facts;  \n}';  \n'GraphTraversalCorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:GetCausallyDependentFactsFromGraphCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nGraphDef ::= 'record', 'Graph', Identifier, '{'  \n'Vertices' : Set(Type),  \n'Edges' : Set(Pair(Type, Type))  \n'}';  \nGraphDef.empty ::= 'Rule((), \"=\\\u003e\", GraphDef)';  \nGraphDef.empty := 'λ \\_ . GraphDef(Vertices = Rule.execute(Set.empty, UnitType).Output.Set, Edges = Rule.execute(Set.empty, UnitType).Output.Set)';  \nGraphDef.add\\_vertex ::= 'Rule((G:GraphDef, V:Type), \"=\\\u003e\", GraphDef)';  \nGraphDef.add\\_vertex := 'λ (g, v). GraphDef(Vertices = Rule.execute(Set.add, (g.Vertices, v)).Output.UpdatedSet, Edges = g.Edges)';  \nGraphDef.add\\_edge ::= 'Rule((G:GraphDef, Source:Type, Target:Type), \"=\\\u003e\", GraphDef)';  \nGraphDef.add\\_edge := 'λ (g, src, tgt). GraphDef(Vertices = Rule.execute(Set.union, (Rule.execute(Set.union, (g.Vertices, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, src).Output.Set)), Rule.execute(Set.singleton, tgt).Output.Set)).Output.Set, Edges = Rule.execute(Set.add, (g.Edges, Pair(src, tgt))).Output.Set)';  \nGraphDef.get\\_successors ::= 'Rule((G:GraphDef, V:Type), \"=\\\u003e\", Set(Type))';  \nGraphDef.get\\_successors := 'λ (g, v). {  \nLET successors = Rule.execute(Set.empty, UnitType).Output.Set;  \nFOR\\_EACH edge IN Rule.execute(Set.to\\_list, g.Edges).Output.Sequence {  \nIF edge.first = v THEN  \nsuccessors = Rule.execute(Set.add, (successors, edge.second)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nEND IF;  \n};  \nRETURN successors;  \n}';  \n  \nEpistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp ::= 'record', 'EpistemicAcquireKnowledgeOperation', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(OldEpistemicState:EpistemicStateDef, NewFact:Proposition, AssociatedResourceID:Plan9ResourceID, KnowledgeHash:StringDef, SystemState:SystemStateDef)',  \n    'Output' : '(UpdatedEpistemicState:EpistemicStateDef, KnowledgeAcquired:Boolean)',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET current\\_facts = input.OldEpistemicState.Facts;  \n        LET current\\_derivation\\_graph = input.OldEpistemicState.DerivationGraph;  \n        LET new\\_facts\\_derived, updated\\_derivation\\_graph = Rule.execute(InferenceEngineDef.Deduce, (Rule.execute(Set.add, (current\\_facts, input.NewFact)).Output.UpdatedSet, s\\_curr.InferenceEngine.RuleSet)).Output;  \n        LET knowledge\\_acquired = Not(Rule.execute(Set.member, (input.NewFact, current\\_facts)).Output.Boolean);  \n  \n        LET updated\\_epistemic\\_state = EpistemicStateDef(  \n            ObserverIdentity = input.OldEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity,  \n            PerceptionBoundaries = input.OldEpistemicState.PerceptionBoundaries,  \n            BeliefRevisionHistory = Rule.execute(Sequence.append, (input.OldEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory, Rule.execute(Sequence.singleton, BeliefStateSnapshot(  \n                Timestamp = Rule.execute(CurrentTimestamp, UnitType).Output.RealDef,  \n                FactsBefore = input.OldEpistemicState.Facts,  \n                FactsAfter = Rule.execute(Set.union, (current\\_facts, new\\_facts\\_derived)).Output.UpdatedSet,  \n                OperationDetails = input.NewFact,  \n                AssociatedResource = input.AssociatedResourceID,  \n                KnowledgeHash = input.KnowledgeHash  \n            )).Output.Sequence)).Output.Sequence,  \n            CurrentContext = input.OldEpistemicState.CurrentContext,  \n            Facts = Rule.execute(Set.union, (current\\_facts, new\\_facts\\_derived)).Output.UpdatedSet,  \n            DerivationGraph = updated\\_derivation\\_graph  \n        );  \n        RETURN (updated\\_epistemic\\_state, knowledge\\_acquired);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET last\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Sequence.last, input.OldEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(last\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET last\\_snapshot = last\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n        ASSERT last\\_snapshot.AssociatedResource = input.AssociatedResourceID;  \n        ASSERT last\\_snapshot.KnowledgeHash = input.KnowledgeHash;  \n  \n        LET restored\\_facts = last\\_snapshot.FactsBefore;  \n        LET final\\_restored\\_facts = Rule.execute(InferenceEngineDef.Retract, (input.OldEpistemicState.Facts, input.NewFact, s\\_post.InferenceEngine.RuleSet, input.OldEpistemicState.DerivationGraph)).Output.UpdatedFacts;  \n  \n  \n        LET restored\\_epistemic\\_state = EpistemicStateDef(  \n            ObserverIdentity = input.OldEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity,  \n            PerceptionBoundaries = input.OldEpistemicState.PerceptionBoundaries,  \n            BeliefRevisionHistory = Rule.execute(Sequence.remove\\_last, input.OldEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory).Output.Sequence,  \n            CurrentContext = input.OldEpistemicState.CurrentContext,  \n            Facts = final\\_restored\\_facts,  \n            DerivationGraph = input.OldEpistemicState.DerivationGraph  \n        );  \n        RETURN (input, restored\\_epistemic\\_state);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule((Input:Self.Input, InitialState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, s\\_init). {  \n        LET old\\_epistemic\\_state = input.OldEpistemicState;  \n        RETURN ChangeDeltaDef(  \n            EpistemicStatesDelta = Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (old\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, OldEpistemicSnapshotDef(old\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, old\\_epistemic\\_state))).Output.Map  \n        );  \n    }',  \n    'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule((Input:Self.Input, InitialState:SystemStateDef, Output:Self.Output, FinalState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, s\\_init, output, s\\_final). {  \n        LET new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_final.EpistemicStates, input.OldEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity)).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET new\\_epistemic\\_state = new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt.Value;  \n        RETURN ChangeDeltaDef(  \n            EpistemicStatesDelta = Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (new\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, NewEpistemicSnapshotDef(new\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, new\\_epistemic\\_state))).Output.Map  \n        );  \n    }',  \n    'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicAcquireKnowledgeOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n    'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicAcquireKnowledgeOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n    'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicAcquireKnowledgeOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n    'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicAcquireKnowledgeOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nEpistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp ::= 'record', 'EpistemicRevokeKnowledgeOperation', '{'  \n    'Input' : '(CurrentEpistemicState:EpistemicStateDef, FactToRevoke:Proposition, AssociatedResourceID:Plan9ResourceID, SystemState:SystemStateDef)',  \n    'Output' : '(UpdatedEpistemicState:EpistemicStateDef, KnowledgeRevoked:Boolean)',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET current\\_facts = input.CurrentEpistemicState.Facts;  \n        LET current\\_derivation\\_graph = input.CurrentEpistemicState.DerivationGraph;  \n  \n        LET knowledge\\_revoked = Rule.execute(Set.member, (input.FactToRevoke, current\\_facts)).Output.Boolean;  \n  \n        LET updated\\_facts = Rule.execute(InferenceEngineDef.Retract, (current\\_facts, input.FactToRevoke, s\\_curr.InferenceEngine.RuleSet, current\\_derivation\\_graph)).Output.UpdatedFacts;  \n          \n        LET updated\\_epistemic\\_state = EpistemicStateDef(  \n            ObserverIdentity = input.CurrentEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity,  \n            PerceptionBoundaries = input.CurrentEpistemicState.PerceptionBoundaries,  \n            BeliefRevisionHistory = Rule.execute(Sequence.append, (input.CurrentEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory, Rule.execute(Sequence.singleton, BeliefStateSnapshot(  \n                Timestamp = Rule.execute(CurrentTimestamp, UnitType).Output.RealDef,  \n                FactsBefore = input.CurrentEpistemicState.Facts,  \n                FactsAfter = updated\\_facts,  \n                OperationDetails = input.FactToRevoke,  \n                AssociatedResource = input.AssociatedResourceID,  \n                KnowledgeHash = StringDef(\"Revoke\")  \n            )).Output.Sequence)).Output.Sequence,  \n            CurrentContext = input.CurrentEpistemicState.CurrentContext,  \n            Facts = updated\\_facts,  \n            DerivationGraph = current\\_derivation\\_graph  \n        );  \n        RETURN (updated\\_epistemic\\_state, knowledge\\_revoked);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET last\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Sequence.last, input.CurrentEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(last\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET last\\_snapshot = last\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n        ASSERT last\\_snapshot.AssociatedResource = input.AssociatedResourceID;  \n  \n        LET restored\\_facts = last\\_snapshot.FactsBefore;  \n          \n        LET restored\\_epistemic\\_state = EpistemicStateDef(  \n            ObserverIdentity = input.CurrentEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity,  \n            PerceptionBoundaries = input.CurrentEpistemicState.PerceptionBoundaries,  \n            BeliefRevisionHistory = Rule.execute(Sequence.remove\\_last, input.CurrentEpistemicState.BeliefRevisionHistory).Output.Sequence,  \n            CurrentContext = input.CurrentEpistemicState.CurrentContext,  \n            Facts = restored\\_facts,  \n            DerivationGraph = input.CurrentEpistemicState.DerivationGraph  \n        );  \n        RETURN (input, restored\\_epistemic\\_state);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule((Input:Self.Input, InitialState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, s\\_init). {  \n        LET old\\_epistemic\\_state = input.CurrentEpistemicState;  \n        RETURN ChangeDeltaDef(  \n            EpistemicStatesDelta = Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (old\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, OldEpistemicSnapshotDef(old\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, old\\_epistemic\\_state))).Output.Map  \n        );  \n    }',  \n    'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule((Input:Self.Input, InitialState:SystemStateDef, Output:Self.Output, FinalState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef)',  \n    'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, s\\_init, output, s\\_final). {  \n        LET new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_final.EpistemicStates, input.CurrentEpistemicState.ObserverIdentity)).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET new\\_epistemic\\_state = new\\_epistemic\\_state\\_opt.Value;  \n        RETURN ChangeDeltaDef(  \n            EpistemicStatesDelta = Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (new\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, NewEpistemicSnapshotDef(new\\_epistemic\\_state.ObserverIdentity, new\\_epistemic\\_state))).Output.Map  \n        );  \n    }',  \n    'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicRevokeKnowledgeOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n    'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicRevokeKnowledgeOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n    'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicRevokeKnowledgeOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n    'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:EpistemicRevokeKnowledgeOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \nBeliefStateSnapshot ::= 'record', 'BeliefStateSnapshot', Identifier, '{'  \n    'Timestamp' : RealDef,  \n    'FactsBefore' : Set(Proposition),  \n    'FactsAfter' : Set(Proposition),  \n    'OperationDetails' : Proposition,  \n    'AssociatedResource' : Plan9ResourceID,  \n    'KnowledgeHash' : StringDef  \n'}';  \n        LET proc\\_dir\\_id = Rule.execute(UUID.generate\\_deterministic, StringDef(\"/proc\")).Output.UUIDType;  \n        LET sys\\_dir\\_id = Rule.execute(UUID.generate\\_deterministic, StringDef(\"/sys\")).Output.UUIDType;  \n  \n        LET initial\\_root\\_entries = Rule.execute(Map.from\\_list, Sequence(  \n            Pair(StringDef(\"proc\"), proc\\_dir\\_id),  \n            Pair(StringDef(\"sys\"), sys\\_dir\\_id)  \n        )).Output.Map;  \n  \n        LET root\\_dir = DirectoryDef(ID=root\\_id, Entries=initial\\_root\\_entries, Parent=None());  \n        LET proc\\_dir = DirectoryDef(ID=proc\\_dir\\_id, Entries=Rule.execute(Map.empty, UnitType).Output.Map, Parent=Some(root\\_id));  \n        LET sys\\_dir = DirectoryDef(ID=sys\\_dir\\_id, Entries=Rule.execute(Map.empty, UnitType).Output.Map, Parent=Some(root\\_id));  \n  \n        RETURN root\\_dir;  \n    }';  \n    'CorrectnessProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:InitialSystemFilesystemTreeCorrectness\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.open-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:open-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:file-handle-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:pid-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-process-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-process-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:rule:file-update-content-v1.0 urn:uuid:rule:file-restore-content-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Open Operation.\"  \n  \nOpenOp ::= 'record', 'OpenOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n'Input' : '(ID:UUID, PathToOpen:PathDef, Mode:ModeDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, TargetProcessID:PIDDef)',  \n'Output' : '(FileHandle:FileHandleDef, FileID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \nLET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToOpen))).Output.Result;  \nASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \nLET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \nLET target\\_file\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \nASSERT IsSome(target\\_file\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(target\\_file\\_opt.Value, FileDef).Output.Boolean;  \nLET target\\_file = target\\_file\\_opt.Value;  \nLET new\\_handle\\_id = Rule.execute(UUID.generate, UnitType).Output.NewUUID;  \nLET new\\_file\\_handle = FileHandleDef(  \nID = new\\_handle\\_id,  \nAssociatedFileID = target\\_file.ID,  \nMode = input.Mode,  \nOffset = 0  \n);  \nLET target\\_process\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.ProcessTable, input.TargetProcessID)).Output.Option;  \nASSERT IsSome(target\\_process\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \nLET target\\_process = target\\_process\\_opt.Value;  \nLET updated\\_handles = Rule.execute(Set.add, (target\\_process.FileHandles, new\\_file\\_handle)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nLET updated\\_process = ProcessDef(  \nID = target\\_process.ID,  \nState = target\\_process.State,  \nMemoryMap = target\\_process.MemoryMap,  \nFileHandles = updated\\_handles  \n);  \nLET sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_process\\_table, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.ProcessTable, input.TargetProcessID, updated\\_process)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState;  \nLET (updated\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value, Proposition(\"File \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToOpen) ++ \" opened\"), target\\_file.ID, Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, target\\_file.Content).Output.StringDef, s\\_curr)).Output;  \nLET final\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState;  \nRETURN (Pair(new\\_file\\_handle, target\\_file.ID), final\\_sys\\_state);  \n}',  \n'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output, input, s\\_post). {  \nLET file\\_handle = output.first;  \nLET target\\_file\\_id = output.second;  \nLET target\\_process\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.ProcessTable, input.TargetProcessID)).Output.Option;  \nASSERT IsSome(target\\_process\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \nLET target\\_process = target\\_process\\_opt.Value;  \nLET updated\\_handles = Rule.execute(Set.remove, (target\\_process.FileHandles, file\\_handle)).Output.UpdatedSet;  \nLET updated\\_process = ProcessDef(  \nID = target\\_process.ID,  \nState = target\\_process.State,  \nMemoryMap = target\\_process.MemoryMap,  \nFileHandles = updated\\_handles  \n);  \nLET sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_process\\_table, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.ProcessTable, input.TargetProcessID, updated\\_process)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState;  \nLET (restored\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value, Proposition(\"File \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToOpen) ++ \" opened\"), target\\_file\\_id, s\\_post)).Output;  \nLET final\\_sys\\_state\\_restored = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_update.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState;  \nRETURN (input, final\\_sys\\_state\\_restored);  \n}',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, input.TargetProcessID).Output.Set, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:OpenOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:OpenOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:OpenOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:OpenOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.read-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:read-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:file-handle-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Read Operation.\"  \n  \nReadOp ::= 'record', 'ReadOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n'Input' : '(ID:UUID, FileHandle:FileHandleDef, Offset:Nat, Count:Nat, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n'Output' : 'Data:DataStreamDef',  \n'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \nLET target\\_file = Rule.execute(GetFileFromHandle, (input.FileHandle, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.File;  \nLET read\\_data = Rule.execute(GetFileData, (target\\_file.Content, input.Offset, input.Count)).Output.Data;  \nLET read\\_data\\_hash = Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, read\\_data).Output.StringDef;  \nLET (updated\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value, Proposition(\"File \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(Rule.execute(Path.from\\_resource\\_id, (input.RootTreeID, target\\_file.ID, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.ResourcePath.Value) ++ \" content read\"), target\\_file.ID, read\\_data\\_hash, s\\_curr)).Output;  \nRETURN (read\\_data, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n}',  \n'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_data, input, s\\_post). {  \nLET target\\_file = Rule.execute(GetFileFromHandle, (input.FileHandle, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output.File;  \nLET (restored\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value, Proposition(\"File \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(Rule.execute(Path.from\\_resource\\_id, (input.RootTreeID, target\\_file.ID, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output.ResourcePath.Value) ++ \" content read\"), target\\_file.ID, s\\_post)).Output;  \nRETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.write-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:write-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:file-handle-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Write Operation.\"  \n  \nWriteOp ::= 'record', 'WriteOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n'Input' : '(ID:UUID, FileHandle:FileHandleDef, Offset:Nat, Data:DataStreamDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n'Output' : 'BytesWritten:Nat',  \n'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \nLET target\\_file = Rule.execute(GetFileFromHandle, (input.FileHandle, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.File;  \nLET old\\_content\\_segment = Rule.execute(GetFileData, (target\\_file.Content, input.Offset, Rule.execute(Sequence.length, input.Data).Output.Nat)).Output.Data;  \nLET (updated\\_file, sys\\_state\\_after\\_write\\_file) = Rule.execute(File.UpdateContentRule, (target\\_file, input.Data, input.Offset, s\\_curr)).Output;  \nLET content\\_hash = Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, input.Data).Output.StringDef;  \nLET (updated\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value, Proposition(\"Content written to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(Rule.execute(Path.from\\_resource\\_id, (input.RootTreeID, updated\\_file.ID, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.ResourcePath.Value) ++ \" with hash \" ++ content\\_hash), updated\\_file.ID, content\\_hash, s\\_curr)).Output;  \nRETURN (Rule.execute(Sequence.length, input.Data).Output.Nat, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_write\\_file.SysState, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_write\\_file.SysState.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n}',  \n'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_bytes, input, s\\_post). {  \nLET target\\_file\\_opt\\_snap = Rule.execute(TransactionLog.find\\_entry\\_for\\_operation, (s\\_post.GlobalTransactionLog, input.ObserverID, Self.ID)).Output.Entry;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET mount\\_point\\_dir = mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET formal\\_def\\_opt = Rule.execute(SystemKnowledgeBase.LookupByID, input.FormalDefinitionID).Output.FormalDefinition;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(formal\\_def\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET formal\\_def = formal\\_def\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET generated\\_sem\\_node = Rule.execute(SemanticStructureGenerator.GenerateFromFormalDef, formal\\_def).Output.SemanticNode;  \n        LET new\\_sem\\_node\\_id = generated\\_sem\\_node.ID;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry, \\_) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, input.MountPointPath, Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, new\\_sem\\_node\\_id).Output.StringDef, new\\_sem\\_node\\_id, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (s\\_curr, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n        sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_all\\_resources\\_map, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update\\_entry, Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (new\\_sem\\_node\\_id, generated\\_sem\\_node)).Output.Map)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_epistemic, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Semantic filesystem for formal definition \" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, input.FormalDefinitionID).Output.StringDef ++ \" mounted at \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.MountPointPath) ++ \"/\" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, new\\_sem\\_node\\_id).Output.StringDef),  \n            new\\_sem\\_node\\_id,  \n            StringDef(\"MountSemFS\"),  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (new\\_sem\\_node\\_id, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_epistemic.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_id, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET mount\\_point\\_dir\\_path\\_resolved = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.MountPointPath))).Output.Result.OkType;  \n        LET (removed\\_id, updated\\_tree) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.remove\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (s\\_post.FilesystemTree, mount\\_point\\_dir\\_path\\_resolved, Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, output\\_id).Output.StringDef, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        ASSERT removed\\_id = output\\_id;  \n        LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (s\\_post, updated\\_tree)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n        sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.remove\\_resource\\_from\\_map, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update, output\\_id)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_epistemic, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Semantic filesystem for formal definition \" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, input.FormalDefinitionID).Output.StringDef ++ \" mounted at \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.MountPointPath) ++ \"/\" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, output\\_id).Output.StringDef),  \n            output\\_id,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_epistemic.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_path\\_resolved = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.MountPointPath))).Output.Result.OkType;  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_init.FilesystemTree, mount\\_point\\_path\\_resolved, s\\_init.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_dir = mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, mount\\_point\\_dir.ID).Output.Set, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_path\\_resolved = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.MountPointPath))).Output.Result.OkType;  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_final.FilesystemTree, mount\\_point\\_path\\_resolved, s\\_final.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET mount\\_point\\_dir = mount\\_point\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, Rule.execute(Set.union, (Rule.execute(Set.singleton, mount\\_point\\_dir.ID).Output.Set, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, output).Output.Set)).Output.Set, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:MountSemFSOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:MountSemFSOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:MountSemFSOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:MountSemFSOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.cdsemfs-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:cdsemfs-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Change Semantic Filesystem Directory Operation.\"  \n  \nCdSemFSOp ::= 'record', 'CdSemFSOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(TargetSemFSPath:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'NewEpistemicContext:EpistemicContextDef',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_node\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND (IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean OR IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean);  \n        LET target\\_resource = target\\_node\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET current\\_observer\\_epistemic\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(current\\_observer\\_epistemic\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET current\\_observer\\_epistemic = current\\_observer\\_epistemic\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET new\\_epistemic\\_context = EpistemicContextDef(FocusedResourceID=Some(target\\_resource.ID), CurrentConceptualPath=target\\_abs\\_path);  \n        LET updated\\_epistemic\\_state = Rule.execute(Epistemic.ChangeContext, (current\\_observer\\_epistemic, new\\_epistemic\\_context)).Output.UpdatedEpistemicState;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            current\\_observer\\_epistemic,  \n            Proposition(\"Epistemic context changed to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(target\\_abs\\_path)),  \n            target\\_resource.ID,  \n            StringDef(\"CdSemFS\"),  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n  \n        RETURN (new\\_epistemic\\_context, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_epistemic\\_context, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET log\\_entry\\_opt = Rule.execute(TransactionLog.find\\_entry\\_for\\_operation, (s\\_post.GlobalTransactionLog, input.ObserverID, Self.ID)).Output.Entry;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(log\\_entry\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET prev\\_epistemic\\_state\\_snapshot = log\\_entry\\_opt.Value.PreOpDelta.EpistemicStatesDelta.lookup(input.ObserverID).Value;  \n        LET restored\\_epistemic\\_state = prev\\_epistemic\\_state\\_snapshot.EpistemicState;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Epistemic context changed to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath)),  \n            output\\_epistemic\\_context.FocusedResourceID.Value,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);
