 \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.catsemfs-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:catsemfs-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Concatenate Semantic Filesystem Operation.\"  \n  \nCatSemFSOp ::= 'record', 'CatSemFSOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(TargetSemFSPath:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'FormalStructureRepresentation:Term | Type',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_node\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node = target\\_node\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET formal\\_content\\_opt = Rule.execute(SystemKnowledgeBase.LookupByID, target\\_sem\\_node.FormalContentID).Output.FormalDefinition;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(formal\\_content\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET formal\\_content = formal\\_content\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Inspected formal structure of \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath)),  \n            target\\_sem\\_node.ID,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, StringDef.from\\_formal\\_term\\_or\\_type(formal\\_content)).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (formal\\_content, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_formal\\_structure, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET target\\_node\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_post.FilesystemTree, Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath))).Output.Result.OkType, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node = target\\_node\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Inspected formal structure of \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath)),  \n            target\\_sem\\_node.ID,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.lssemfs-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:lssemfs-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the List Semantic Filesystem Operation.\"  \n  \nLsSemFSOp ::= 'record', 'LsSemFSOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(PathToList:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'OutputData:DataStreamDef',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToList))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_resource = target\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET output\\_string = StringDef(\"\");  \n        IF IsInstanceOf(target\\_resource, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n            LET target\\_dir = target\\_resource;  \n            FOR\\_EACH entry\\_name\\_id\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, target\\_dir.Entries).Output.Sequence {  \n                LET entry\\_name = entry\\_name\\_id\\_pair.first;  \n                output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, entry\\_name ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n            };  \n        ELSE IF IsInstanceOf(target\\_resource, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean THEN  \n            LET sem\\_node = target\\_resource;  \n            output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"Formal Content ID: \" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, sem\\_node.FormalContentID).Output.StringDef ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n            output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"Node Type: \" ++ StringDef.from\\_node\\_type(sem\\_node.NodeType).Output.StringDef ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n            output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"Description: \" ++ sem\\_node.Description ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n            IF Not(Rule.execute(Map.is\\_empty, sem\\_node.ChildNodes).Output.Boolean) THEN  \n                output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"Child Elements:\\\\n\");  \n                FOR\\_EACH child\\_name\\_id\\_pair IN Rule.execute(Map.to\\_list, sem\\_node.ChildNodes).Output.Sequence {  \n                    output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"  \" ++ child\\_name\\_id\\_pair.first ++ \" (ID: \" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, child\\_name\\_id\\_pair.second).Output.StringDef ++ \")\\\\n\");  \n                };  \n            END IF;  \n        ELSE  \n            output\\_string = StringDef.append(output\\_string, \"Not a directory or semantic node: \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToList) ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n        END IF;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Listed semantic filesystem path \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToList)),  \n            target\\_resource.ID,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, StringDef.to\\_datastream(output\\_string)).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (StringDef.to\\_datastream(output\\_string), Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_data, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET target\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToList))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(target\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = target\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_post.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_resource = target\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Listed semantic filesystem path \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.PathToList)),  \n            target\\_resource.ID,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LsSemFSOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LsSemFSOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LsSemFSOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LsSemFSOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.find-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:find-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Find Operation.\"  \n  \nFindOp ::= 'record', 'FindOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(StartPath:PathDef, Predicate:Rule(Plan9Resource =\\\u003e Boolean), MaxDepth:Option(Nat), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'FoundPaths:DataStreamDef',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_start\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.StartPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_start\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET abs\\_start\\_path = resolved\\_start\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET matching\\_resource\\_ids = Rule.execute(RecursiveDirectoryTraversalRule, (abs\\_start\\_path, input.Predicate, input.MaxDepth, s\\_curr)).Output.MatchingResources;  \n  \n        LET found\\_paths\\_string = StringDef(\"\");  \n        FOR\\_EACH resource\\_id IN Rule.execute(Set.to\\_list, matching\\_resource\\_ids).Output.Sequence {  \n            LET resource\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.from\\_resource\\_id, (input.RootTreeID, resource\\_id, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.ResourcePath;  \n            IF IsSome(resource\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean THEN  \n                found\\_paths\\_string = StringDef.append(found\\_paths\\_string, StringDef.from\\_sequence(resource\\_path\\_opt.Value) ++ \"\\\\n\");  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Executed find operation from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.StartPath)),  \n            input.ID,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, StringDef.to\\_datastream(found\\_paths\\_string)).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (StringDef.to\\_datastream(found\\_paths\\_string), Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_data, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Executed find operation from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.StartPath)),  \n            input.ID,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:FindOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:FindOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:FindOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:FindOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.xargs-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:xargs-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:data-stream-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:shell-command-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:nat-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-shell-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:system-state-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Xargs Operation.\"  \n  \nXargsOp ::= 'record', 'XargsOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(InputDataStream:DataStreamDef, CommandToExecute:ShellCommandDef, BatchSize:Option(Nat), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, ShellState:Plan9ShellDef, SysState:SystemStateDef)',  \n    'Output' : 'CombinedOutput:DataStreamDef',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET input\\_lines = Rule.execute(StringDef.split, (StringDef.from\\_datastream(input.InputDataStream), \"\\\\n\")).Output.Sequence;  \n        LET combined\\_output\\_data = StringDef.to\\_datastream(StringDef(\"\"));  \n        LET current\\_s\\_state = s\\_curr;  \n        LET current\\_shell\\_state = input.ShellState;  \n  \n        FOR\\_EACH line IN input\\_lines.Elements {  \n            IF Not(Rule.execute(StringDef.is\\_empty, line).Output.Boolean) THEN  \n                LET args\\_for\\_cmd = Rule.execute(Sequence.append, (input.CommandToExecute.Arguments, Rule.execute(Sequence.singleton, line).Output.Sequence)).Output.Sequence;  \n                LET cmd\\_with\\_args = ShellCommandDef(  \n                    CommandType = input.CommandToExecute.CommandType,  \n                    Arguments = args\\_for\\_cmd,  \n                    RedirectOutput = input.CommandToExecute.RedirectOutput,  \n                    PipeTo = input.CommandToExecute.PipeTo,  \n                    ExecutionID = Rule.execute(UUID.generate, UnitType).Output.NewUUID  \n                );  \n  \n                LET execution\\_result = Rule.execute(Plan9Shell.ExecuteCommand, (cmd\\_with\\_args, current\\_shell\\_state, current\\_s\\_state, input.ObserverID)).Output.Result;  \n                ASSERT IsSuccess(execution\\_result).Output.Boolean;  \n                LET (updated\\_sub\\_shell\\_state, updated\\_sub\\_sys\\_state\\_pair) = execution\\_result.OkType;  \n                LET sub\\_sys\\_state = updated\\_sub\\_sys\\_state\\_pair.first;  \n                LET sub\\_command\\_output = updated\\_sub\\_sys\\_state\\_pair.second;  \n  \n                combined\\_output\\_data = Rule.execute(DataStream.append, (combined\\_output\\_data, sub\\_command\\_output)).Output.UpdatedStream;  \n                current\\_s\\_state = sub\\_sys\\_state;  \n                current\\_shell\\_state = updated\\_sub\\_shell\\_state;  \n            END IF;  \n        };  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Executed xargs operation with command \" ++ StringDef.from\\_command\\_type(input.CommandToExecute.CommandType)),  \n            input.ID,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, combined\\_output\\_data).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (combined\\_output\\_data, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (current\\_s\\_state, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (current\\_s\\_state.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_data, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Executed xargs operation with command \" ++ StringDef.from\\_command\\_type(input.CommandToExecute.CommandType)),  \n            input.ID,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:XargsOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:XargsOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:XargsOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:XargsOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.mv-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:mv-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Move Operation.\"  \n  \nMvOp ::= 'record', 'MoveOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(SourcePath:PathDef, DestinationPath:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'MovedResourceID:Plan9ResourceID',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET abs\\_source\\_path = resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET source\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, abs\\_source\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_resource = source\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET source\\_resource\\_id = source\\_resource.ID;  \n  \n        LET source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_source\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_parent\\_path = source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET source\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, abs\\_source\\_path).Output.LeafName;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_leaf\\_name = source\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET abs\\_dest\\_path = resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_dest\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET dest\\_parent\\_path = dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, abs\\_dest\\_path).Output.LeafName;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET dest\\_leaf\\_name = dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        (\\* Step 1: Add resource to new location \\*)  \n        LET (updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry, \\_) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, dest\\_parent\\_path, dest\\_leaf\\_name, source\\_resource\\_id, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (s\\_curr, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        (\\* Step 2: Remove resource from old location \\*)  \n        LET (removed\\_id, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_remove\\_entry) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.remove\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry.FilesystemTree, source\\_parent\\_path, source\\_leaf\\_name, sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        ASSERT removed\\_id = source\\_resource\\_id;  \n        LET final\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_remove\\_entry)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Moved resource from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath) ++ \" to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath)),  \n            source\\_resource\\_id,  \n            IF IsInstanceOf(source\\_resource, FileDef).Output.Boolean THEN Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, source\\_resource.Content).Output.StringDef ELSE StringDef(\"N/A\") END IF,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (source\\_resource\\_id, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (final\\_sys\\_state, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (final\\_sys\\_state.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_id, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET log\\_entry\\_opt = Rule.execute(TransactionLog.find\\_entry\\_for\\_operation, (s\\_post.GlobalTransactionLog, input.ObserverID, Self.ID)).Output.Entry;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(log\\_entry\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET pre\\_op\\_delta = log\\_entry\\_opt.Value.PreOpDelta;  \n        LET post\\_op\\_delta = log\\_entry\\_opt.Value.PostOpDelta;  \n  \n        LET current\\_sys\\_state = s\\_post;  \n  \n        (\\* Step 1: Remove resource from new location (effectively undoing its creation at dest) \\*)  \n        LET new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (post\\_op\\_delta.FileSystemDelta, output\\_id)).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest = new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest\\_opt.Value;  \n          \n        LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest.LocationPath).Output.ParentPath;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot = dest\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, new\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_dest.LocationPath).Output.LeafName;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot = dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (removed\\_id\\_from\\_dest, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_remove) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.remove\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (current\\_sys\\_state.FilesystemTree, dest\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot, dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot, current\\_sys\\_state.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        ASSERT removed\\_id\\_from\\_dest = output\\_id;  \n        current\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (current\\_sys\\_state, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_remove)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n        current\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.remove\\_resource\\_from\\_map, (current\\_sys\\_state, output\\_id)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n  \n        (\\* Step 2: Add resource back to old location (effectively re-creating it at source) \\*)  \n        LET old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source\\_opt = Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (pre\\_op\\_delta.FileSystemDelta, output\\_id)).Output.Option;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source = old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET source\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.LocationPath).Output.ParentPath;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot = source\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET source\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.leaf, old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.LocationPath).Output.LeafName;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot = source\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        current\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_all\\_resources\\_map, (current\\_sys\\_state, Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (output\\_id,  \n            IF old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.ResourceType = TypeOf(FileDef) THEN FileDef(ID=output\\_id, Content=old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.Content.Value, Metadata=old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.Metadata)  \n            ELSE DirectoryDef(ID=output\\_id, Entries=old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.Entries.Value, Parent=old\\_resource\\_snapshot\\_at\\_source.Parent.Value) END IF  \n        )).Output.Map)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (re\\_added\\_id\\_to\\_source, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (current\\_sys\\_state.FilesystemTree, source\\_parent\\_path\\_from\\_snapshot, source\\_leaf\\_name\\_from\\_snapshot, output\\_id, current\\_sys\\_state.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        ASSERT re\\_added\\_id\\_to\\_source = output\\_id;  \n        current\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (current\\_sys\\_state, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Moved resource from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath) ++ \" to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath)),  \n            output\\_id,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (current\\_sys\\_state, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (current\\_sys\\_state.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    LET resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath))).Output.Result;  \n    ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET abs\\_source\\_path = resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n    LET source\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_init.FilesystemTree, abs\\_source\\_path, s\\_init.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(source\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET source\\_resource = source\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n      \n    LET source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_source\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_path = source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_init.FilesystemTree, source\\_parent\\_path, s\\_init.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_dir = source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n    LET resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath))).Output.Result;  \n    ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET abs\\_dest\\_path = resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_dest\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_path = dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_init.FilesystemTree, dest\\_parent\\_path, s\\_init.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_dir = dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, Rule.execute(Set.union, (Rule.execute(Set.singleton, source\\_resource.ID).Output.Set, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, source\\_parent\\_dir.ID).Output.Set, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, dest\\_parent\\_dir.ID).Output.Set)).Output.Set, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    LET resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath))).Output.Result;  \n    ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET abs\\_source\\_path = resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n    LET resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath))).Output.Result;  \n    ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET abs\\_dest\\_path = resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_source\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_path = source\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_final.FilesystemTree, source\\_parent\\_path, s\\_final.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET source\\_parent\\_dir = source\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_dest\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_path = dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt.Value;  \n    LET dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_final.FilesystemTree, dest\\_parent\\_path, s\\_final.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(dest\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.cp-op-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:cp-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:plan9-resource-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-resource-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Copy Operation.\"  \n  \nCpOp ::= 'record', 'CopyOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(SourcePath:PathDef, DestinationPath:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'CopiedResourceID:Plan9ResourceID',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET abs\\_source\\_path = resolved\\_source\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET source\\_resource\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, abs\\_source\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(source\\_resource\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_resource = source\\_resource\\_opt.Value;  \n        ASSERT IsInstanceOf(source\\_resource, FileDef).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET source\\_file = source\\_resource;  \n  \n        LET new\\_resource\\_id = Rule.execute(UUID.generate, UnitType).Output.NewUUID;  \n        LET new\\_file = FileDef(ID=new\\_resource\\_id, Content=source\\_file.Content, Metadata=source\\_file.Metadata);  \n  \n        LET resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET abs\\_dest\\_path = resolved\\_dest\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt = Rule.execute(Path.parent, abs\\_dest\\_path).Output.ParentPath;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(dest\\_parent\\_path\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET dest\\_leaf\\_name = dest\\_leaf\\_name\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry, \\_) = Rule.execute(DirectoryDef.add\\_entry\\_at\\_path, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, dest\\_parent\\_path, dest\\_leaf\\_name, new\\_resource\\_id, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output;  \n        LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (s\\_curr, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n        LET final\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_all\\_resources\\_map, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry, Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (new\\_resource\\_id, new\\_file)).Output.Map)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Copied resource from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath) ++ \" to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath)),  \n            new\\_resource\\_id,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, new\\_file.Content).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.remove\\_resource\\_from\\_map, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_fs\\_update, output\\_id)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Copied resource from \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.SourcePath) ++ \" to \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.DestinationPath)),  \n            output\\_id,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_filesystem\\_tree, (s\\_curr, updated\\_tree\\_after\\_add\\_entry)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n        LET final\\_sys\\_state = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_all\\_resources\\_map, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_add\\_entry, Rule.execute(Map.singleton, (new\\_link\\_id, new\\_link\\_file)).Output.Map)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n    ASSERT IsSome(link\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt).Output.Boolean AND IsInstanceOf(link\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value, DirectoryDef).Output.Boolean;  \n    LET link\\_parent\\_dir = link\\_parent\\_dir\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, Rule.execute(Set.union, (Rule.execute(Set.singleton, link\\_parent\\_dir.ID).Output.Set, Rule.execute(Set.singleton, output).Output.Set)).Output.Set, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LnOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LnOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LnOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LnOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.doc-synthesis-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:docsynthesis-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Documentation Synthesis Operation.\"  \n  \nDocSynthesisOp ::= 'record', 'DocSynthesisOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n    'ID' : UUID,  \n    'Input' : '(TargetSemFSPath:PathDef, ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, CurrentWorkingDirectory:PathDef, RootTreeID:Plan9ResourceID)',  \n    'Output' : 'DocumentationContent:DataStreamDef',  \n    'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n        LET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_node\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_curr.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_curr.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt = IF IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean THEN Some(target\\_node\\_opt.Value) ELSE None() END IF;  \n  \n        IF IsNone(target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean THEN  \n            RETURN (StringDef.to\\_datastream(StringDef(\"Error: Target is not a semantic node.\")), s\\_curr);  \n        END IF;  \n  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node = target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt.Value;  \n        LET formal\\_content\\_opt = Rule.execute(SystemKnowledgeBase.LookupByID, target\\_sem\\_node.FormalContentID).Output.FormalDefinition;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(formal\\_content\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET formal\\_content = formal\\_content\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET synthesized\\_doc\\_string = Rule.execute(DocumentationSynthesizer.Synthesize, (formal\\_content, target\\_sem\\_node.NodeType, s\\_curr)).Output.StringDef;  \n  \n        LET (updated\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.AcquireKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Synthesized documentation for \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath)),  \n            target\\_sem\\_node.ID,  \n            Rule.execute(ComputeSHA3\\_512, StringDef.to\\_datastream(synthesized\\_doc\\_string)).Output.StringDef,  \n            s\\_curr  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (StringDef.to\\_datastream(synthesized\\_doc\\_string), Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_curr, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_curr.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, updated\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n    'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_data, input, s\\_post). {  \n        LET resolved\\_path\\_res = Rule.execute(Path.resolve, (input.CurrentWorkingDirectory, StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath))).Output.Result;  \n        ASSERT IsSuccess(resolved\\_path\\_res).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_abs\\_path = resolved\\_path\\_res.OkType;  \n  \n        LET target\\_node\\_opt = Rule.execute(GetResourceByPath, (s\\_post.FilesystemTree, target\\_abs\\_path, s\\_post.AllSystemResources)).Output.Resource;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt = IF IsInstanceOf(target\\_node\\_opt.Value, SemanticNodeDef).Output.Boolean THEN Some(target\\_node\\_opt.Value) ELSE None() END IF;  \n        ASSERT IsSome(target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt).Output.Boolean;  \n        LET target\\_sem\\_node = target\\_sem\\_node\\_opt.Value;  \n  \n        LET (restored\\_observer\\_knowledge, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n            Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n            Proposition(\"Synthesized documentation for \" ++ StringDef.from\\_sequence(input.TargetSemFSPath)),  \n            target\\_sem\\_node.ID,  \n            s\\_post  \n        )).Output;  \n        RETURN (input, Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (s\\_post, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (s\\_post.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer\\_knowledge.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState);  \n    }',  \n    'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:DocSynthesisOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:DocSynthesisOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:DocSynthesisOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:DocSynthesisOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';  \n  \n\\--PLAN9\\_MODEL\\_LIBRARY\\_MANIFEST--  \nContent-Type: application/vnd.system-pi.reversible-operation.launch-shell-v1.0; charset=\"UTF-8\"  \nMessage-ID: urn:uuid:op:launchshell-v1.0  \nReferences: urn:uuid:typedef:observer-id-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:path-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-plan9-shell-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-system-state-management-v1.0 urn:uuid:lib-epistemic-inference-v1.0-verified urn:uuid:lib-uuid-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:change-delta-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-process-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-process-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:old-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0 urn:uuid:typedef:new-epistemic-snapshot-v1.0  \nContent-Description: \"Formal definition of the Launch Shell Operation.\"  \n  \nLaunchShellOp ::= 'record', 'LaunchShellOperation', Identifier, 'extends', ReversibleOperationDef, '{'  \n  'ID' : UUID,  \n  'Input' : '(ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialWorkingDirectory:PathDef)',  \n  'Output' : 'ShellInstance:Plan9ShellDef',  \n  'ForwardOp' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Output:Self.Output, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n  'ForwardOp' := 'λ (input, s\\_curr). {  \n      LET (initial\\_shell\\_state, updated\\_s\\_state) = Rule.execute(Plan9Shell.BootstrapShellState, (s\\_curr, input.ObserverID)).Output;  \n      RETURN (initial\\_shell\\_state, updated\\_s\\_state);  \n  }',  \n  'BackwardOp' : 'Rule( (Output:Self.Output, Input:Self.Input, SysState:SystemStateDef), \"=\\\u003e\", (Input:Self.Input, SysState':SystemStateDef) )',  \n  'BackwardOp' := 'λ (output\\_shell, input, s\\_post). {  \n      (\\* Reversing shell launch means terminating the shell process and revoking its epistemic traces. \\*)  \n      LET terminated\\_process = ProcessDef(  \n          ID = output\\_shell.ShellProcess.ID,  \n          State = Terminated,  \n          MemoryMap = output\\_shell.ShellProcess.MemoryMap,  \n          FileHandles = output\\_shell.ShellProcess.FileHandles  \n      );  \n      LET updated\\_process\\_table = Rule.execute(Map.remove, (s\\_post.ProcessTable, terminated\\_process.ID)).Output.UpdatedMap;  \n      LET sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_term = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_process\\_table, (s\\_post, updated\\_process\\_table)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n      LET (restored\\_observer, \\_) = Rule.execute(Epistemic.RevokeKnowledgeOp, (  \n          Rule.execute(Map.lookup, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_term.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID)).Output.Option.Value,  \n          Proposition(\"Shell launched with ID: \" ++ Rule.execute(UUID.to\\_string, output\\_shell.ShellProcess.ID).Output.StringDef),  \n          output\\_shell.ShellProcess.ID,  \n          sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_term  \n      )).Output;  \n      LET final\\_sys\\_state\\_restored = Rule.execute(SystemStateDef.update\\_epistemic\\_states, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_term, Rule.execute(Map.insert\\_or\\_update, (sys\\_state\\_after\\_process\\_term.EpistemicStates, input.ObserverID, restored\\_observer.CognitiveState)).Output.UpdatedMap)).Output.UpdatedState;  \n  \n      RETURN (input, final\\_sys\\_state\\_restored);  \n  }',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, InitialSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PreOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPreOpDelta, (input, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_init, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' : 'Rule( (Input:Self.Input, Output:Self.Output, RelevantResourceIDs:Set(Plan9ResourceID), ChangedProcessIDs:Set(PIDDef), ChangedNetworkIDs:Set(UUID), ChangedDeviceIDs:Set(DeviceIDDef), ObserverID:ObserverIDDef, FinalSysState:SystemStateDef, ChangedMountIDs:Set(UUID)), \"=\\\\\\\u003e\", ChangeDeltaDef )',  \n'PostOpDeltaExtractor' := 'λ (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids). {  \n    RETURN Rule.execute(CommonDeltaExtractorTemplate.ExtractPostOpDelta, (input, output, res\\_ids, proc\\_ids, net\\_ids, dev\\_ids, obs\\_id, s\\_final, mount\\_ids)).Output;  \n}',  \n'IdentityAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LaunchShellOpIdentity\\_20250724)',  \n'InverseAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LaunchShellOpInverse\\_20250724)',  \n'InvolutionAxiom' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LaunchShellOpInvolution\\_20250724)',  \n'DeltaConsistencyProof' : 'ProofObjectHandle(urn:uuid:proof:LaunchShellOpDeltaConsistency\\_20250724)'  \n'}';
